					check_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_variant = constant:natural_disaster_relief_variant.port_lifeline }
					is_coastal = yes
				}
				AND = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_variant = constant:natural_disaster_relief_variant.engineer }
					OR = {
						natural_disaster_state_has_strategic_transport = yes
						natural_disaster_state_has_industry = yes
					}
				}
				AND = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_variant = constant:natural_disaster_relief_variant.medical }
					OR = {
						natural_disaster_state_has_dense_population = yes
						has_state_flag = natural_disaster_disease_pressure
					}
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_score = 0 }
		natural_disaster_score_priority_state = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_score > natural_disaster_priority_best_score } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_best_score = natural_disaster_priority_score }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_selected = 1 }
			save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_priority_open_card_state
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_resolve_call_severity = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_type = constant:natural_disaster_caller.random }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_type = constant:natural_disaster_caller.cluster }
			}
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.local }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_i_stage } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution_i_severe_chance = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.evolution_i_severe_chance }
			random = {
				chance = natural_disaster_evolution_i_severe_chance
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.severe }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_ii_stage } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.regional }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_iii_stage } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.abnormal }
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_resolve_sequence_count = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_count > 0 } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_hits = natural_disaster_call_sequence_count }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_mode = constant:natural_disaster_sequence_mode.single } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_hits = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_mode = constant:natural_disaster_sequence_mode.barrage } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_intensity = constant:natural_disaster_scenario_intensity.maximum } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_hits = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.barrage_maximum_hits }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_intensity = constant:natural_disaster_scenario_intensity.high } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_hits = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.barrage_high_hits }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_intensity = constant:natural_disaster_scenario_intensity.medium } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_hits = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.barrage_medium_hits }
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_hits = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.barrage_low_hits }
		}
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_minimum = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.baseline_min_hits }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_ceiling = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.baseline_max_hits }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_iii_stage } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_minimum = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.evolution_iii_min_hits }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_ceiling = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.evolution_iii_max_hits }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_ii_stage } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_minimum = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.evolution_ii_min_hits }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_ceiling = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.evolution_ii_max_hits }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_i_stage } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_minimum = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.evolution_i_min_hits }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_ceiling = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.evolution_i_max_hits }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_ceiling = 1 }
		set_temp_variable_to_random = {
			var = natural_disaster_sequence_hits
			min = natural_disaster_random_minimum
			max = natural_disaster_random_ceiling
			integer = yes
		}
	}
	round_temp_variable = natural_disaster_sequence_hits
}

# ============================================================================
# PERSISTENT DELAYED JOB QUEUE
# ============================================================================

natural_disaster_reserve_job_day = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_due_date = natural_disaster_job_desired_due_date }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_day_conflict = 1 }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_job_day_conflict > 0 } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_day_conflict = 0 }
		for_loop_effect = {
			start = 0
			end = global.natural_disaster_reserved_sequence_entries^num
			value = i
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						var = global.natural_disaster_reserved_sequence_entries^i
						value = natural_disaster_job_sequence_id
						compare = equals
					}
					check_variable = {
						var = global.natural_disaster_reserved_day_entries^i
						value = natural_disaster_job_due_date
						compare = equals
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_day_conflict = 1 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_job_day_conflict > 0 } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_due_date = 1 }
		}
	}
	add_to_array = {
		array = global.natural_disaster_reserved_sequence_entries
		value = natural_disaster_job_sequence_id
	}
	add_to_array = {
		array = global.natural_disaster_reserved_day_entries
		value = natural_disaster_job_due_date
	}
}

natural_disaster_release_job_day = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_reservation_index = constant:natural_disaster_threshold.invalid_array_index }
	for_loop_effect = {
		start = 0
		end = global.natural_disaster_reserved_sequence_entries^num
		value = i
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_reservation_index < 0 }
				check_variable = {
					var = global.natural_disaster_reserved_sequence_entries^i
					value = natural_disaster_current_job_sequence_id
					compare = equals
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = global.natural_disaster_reserved_day_entries^i
					value = natural_disaster_current_job_due_date
					compare = equals
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_reservation_index = i }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_reservation_index > constant:natural_disaster_threshold.invalid_array_index } }
		remove_from_array = {
			array = global.natural_disaster_reserved_sequence_entries
			index = natural_disaster_reservation_index
		}
		remove_from_array = {
			array = global.natural_disaster_reserved_day_entries
			index = natural_disaster_reservation_index
		}
	}
}

# State scope. Inputs are natural_disaster_job_type and
# natural_disaster_job_delay_days. The state owns persistent disaster payload;
# its current controller owns only the delayed queue row and worker wakeup.
natural_disaster_prepare_job_outcome_snapshot = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_deaths_snapshot = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_damage_transport_snapshot = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_damage_industry_snapshot = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_damage_ports_snapshot = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_damage_air_snapshot = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_damage_resources_snapshot = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_damage_shelter_snapshot = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_warning_choice_snapshot = constant:natural_disaster_warning_choice.none }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_warning_outcome_snapshot = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_warning_matched_snapshot = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_phase_snapshot = constant:natural_disaster_phase.warning }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_segment_snapshot = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_chain_due_date_snapshot = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_chain_target_state_snapshot = THIS }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_has_chain_target_snapshot = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_origin_family_snapshot = constant:natural_disaster_family.random }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_origin_medium_snapshot = constant:natural_disaster_origin_medium.none }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_skyfall_variant_snapshot = constant:natural_disaster_skyfall_variant.none }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_path_basin_snapshot = constant:natural_disaster_path_basin.none }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_last_deaths } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_deaths_snapshot = natural_disaster_last_deaths } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_damage_transport } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_damage_transport_snapshot = natural_disaster_damage_transport } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_damage_industry } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_damage_industry_snapshot = natural_disaster_damage_industry } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_damage_ports } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_damage_ports_snapshot = natural_disaster_damage_ports } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_damage_air } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_damage_air_snapshot = natural_disaster_damage_air } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_damage_resources } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_damage_resources_snapshot = natural_disaster_damage_resources } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_damage_shelter } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_damage_shelter_snapshot = natural_disaster_damage_shelter } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_warning_choice } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_warning_choice_snapshot = natural_disaster_warning_choice } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_warning_outcome } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_warning_outcome_snapshot = natural_disaster_warning_outcome } }
	if = { limit = { natural_disaster_warning_protection_matches_chain = yes } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_warning_matched_snapshot = 1 } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_phase } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_phase_snapshot = natural_disaster_phase } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_path_segment_index } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_segment_snapshot = natural_disaster_path_segment_index } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_chain_due_date } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_chain_due_date_snapshot = natural_disaster_chain_due_date } }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_chain_target_state }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_chain_target_state_snapshot = natural_disaster_chain_target_state }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_has_chain_target_snapshot = 1 }
	}
	if = { limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_sequence_origin_family } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_origin_family_snapshot = natural_disaster_sequence_origin_family } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_origin_medium } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_origin_medium_snapshot = natural_disaster_origin_medium } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_skyfall_variant } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_skyfall_variant_snapshot = natural_disaster_skyfall_variant } }
	if = { limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_path_basin } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_path_basin_snapshot = natural_disaster_path_basin } }
}

natural_disaster_enqueue_state_job = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_sequence_id = natural_disaster_sequence_id }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_family_snapshot = natural_disaster_family }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_followup_route_snapshot = natural_disaster_followup_route }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_severity_snapshot = natural_disaster_severity }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_deaths_snapshot = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_damage_transport_snapshot = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_damage_industry_snapshot = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_damage_ports_snapshot = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_damage_air_snapshot = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_damage_resources_snapshot = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_damage_shelter_snapshot = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_warning_choice_snapshot = constant:natural_disaster_warning_choice.none }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_warning_outcome_snapshot = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_warning_matched_snapshot = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_phase_snapshot = constant:natural_disaster_phase.warning }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_segment_snapshot = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_chain_due_date_snapshot = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_chain_target_state_snapshot = THIS }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_has_chain_target_snapshot = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_origin_family_snapshot = constant:natural_disaster_family.random }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_origin_medium_snapshot = constant:natural_disaster_origin_medium.none }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_skyfall_variant_snapshot = constant:natural_disaster_skyfall_variant.none }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_path_basin_snapshot = constant:natural_disaster_path_basin.none }
	natural_disaster_prepare_job_outcome_snapshot = yes
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_desired_due_date = global.date }
	add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_desired_due_date = natural_disaster_job_delay_days }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_due_date = natural_disaster_job_desired_due_date }
	natural_disaster_reserve_job_day = yes
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_effective_delay = natural_disaster_job_due_date }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_effective_delay = global.date }
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = natural_disaster_job_effective_delay
		min = constant:natural_disaster_threshold.minimum_job_delay_days
	}
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_next_subevent_date = natural_disaster_job_due_date }
	controller = {
		add_to_array = {
			array = natural_disaster_job_target_state_entries
			value = PREV
		}
		add_to_array = {
			array = natural_disaster_job_type_entries
			value = natural_disaster_job_type
		}
		add_to_array = {
			array = natural_disaster_job_sequence_id_entries
			value = natural_disaster_job_sequence_id
		}
		add_to_array = {
			array = natural_disaster_job_due_date_entries
			value = natural_disaster_job_due_date
		}
		add_to_array = {
			array = natural_disaster_job_family_entries
			value = natural_disaster_job_family_snapshot
		}
		add_to_array = {
			array = natural_disaster_job_followup_route_entries
			value = natural_disaster_job_followup_route_snapshot
		}
		add_to_array = {
			array = natural_disaster_job_severity_entries
			value = natural_disaster_job_severity_snapshot
		}
		add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_deaths_entries value = natural_disaster_job_deaths_snapshot }
		add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_damage_transport_entries value = natural_disaster_job_damage_transport_snapshot }
		add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_damage_industry_entries value = natural_disaster_job_damage_industry_snapshot }
		add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_damage_ports_entries value = natural_disaster_job_damage_ports_snapshot }
		add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_damage_air_entries value = natural_disaster_job_damage_air_snapshot }
		add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_damage_resources_entries value = natural_disaster_job_damage_resources_snapshot }
		add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_damage_shelter_entries value = natural_disaster_job_damage_shelter_snapshot }
		add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_warning_choice_entries value = natural_disaster_job_warning_choice_snapshot }
		add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_warning_outcome_entries value = natural_disaster_job_warning_outcome_snapshot }
		add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_warning_matched_entries value = natural_disaster_job_warning_matched_snapshot }
		add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_phase_entries value = natural_disaster_job_phase_snapshot }
		add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_segment_entries value = natural_disaster_job_segment_snapshot }
		add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_chain_due_date_entries value = natural_disaster_job_chain_due_date_snapshot }
		add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_chain_target_state_entries value = natural_disaster_job_chain_target_state_snapshot }
		add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_has_chain_target_entries value = natural_disaster_job_has_chain_target_snapshot }
		add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_origin_family_entries value = natural_disaster_job_origin_family_snapshot }
		add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_origin_medium_entries value = natural_disaster_job_origin_medium_snapshot }
		add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_skyfall_variant_entries value = natural_disaster_job_skyfall_variant_snapshot }
		add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_path_basin_entries value = natural_disaster_job_path_basin_snapshot }
		set_country_flag = natural_disaster_queue_active
		country_event = {
			id = chaosx.nr13.2
			days = natural_disaster_job_effective_delay
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_execute_warning = {
	event_target:natural_disaster_impact_state = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_scheduled
				has_state_flag = natural_disaster_impact_scheduled
			}
			clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_scheduled
			set_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_active
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.warning }
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.warning }
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_path_status = constant:natural_disaster_path_status.warning }
			natural_disaster_set_category_display_from_state = yes
			controller = {
				set_country_flag = natural_disaster_warning_unread
				set_country_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_category_visible
			}
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_fire_family_report = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_report_event_id = 0 }
	event_target:natural_disaster_impact_state = {
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = natural_disaster_current_job_family }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity = natural_disaster_current_job_severity }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_report_followup_route = natural_disaster_current_job_followup_route }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_report_snapshot_severity = natural_disaster_current_job_severity }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_report_snapshot_deaths = natural_disaster_current_job_deaths }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_report_snapshot_damage_transport = natural_disaster_current_job_damage_transport }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_report_snapshot_damage_industry = natural_disaster_current_job_damage_industry }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_report_snapshot_damage_ports = natural_disaster_current_job_damage_ports }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_report_snapshot_damage_air = natural_disaster_current_job_damage_air }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_report_snapshot_damage_resources = natural_disaster_current_job_damage_resources }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_report_snapshot_damage_shelter = natural_disaster_current_job_damage_shelter }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_report_snapshot_warning_choice = natural_disaster_current_job_warning_choice }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_report_snapshot_warning_outcome = natural_disaster_current_job_warning_outcome }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_report_snapshot_warning_matched = natural_disaster_current_job_warning_matched }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_report_snapshot_phase = natural_disaster_current_job_phase }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_report_snapshot_sequence_id = natural_disaster_current_job_sequence_id }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_report_snapshot_segment = natural_disaster_current_job_segment }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_report_snapshot_report_date = natural_disaster_current_job_due_date }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_report_snapshot_followup_due_date = natural_disaster_current_job_chain_due_date }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_report_snapshot_has_linked_state = natural_disaster_current_job_has_chain_target }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_report_snapshot_origin_family = natural_disaster_current_job_origin_family }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_report_snapshot_origin_medium = natural_disaster_current_job_origin_medium }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_report_snapshot_skyfall_variant = natural_disaster_current_job_skyfall_variant }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_report_snapshot_path_basin = natural_disaster_current_job_path_basin }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_has_chain_target > 0 } }
			var:natural_disaster_current_job_chain_target_state = { save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_report_linked_state }
		}
		natural_disaster_prepare_family_profile = yes
		natural_disaster_prepare_severity_profile = yes
		save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_report_state
		set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_report_family = natural_disaster_current_family }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_report_policy = natural_disaster_report_policy }
		controller = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { nd_achievement_report_policy = constant:natural_disaster_report_policy.affected_country }
						check_variable = { nd_achievement_report_policy = constant:natural_disaster_report_policy.delayed_batch }
					}
				}
				natural_disaster_achievement_record_report_group = yes
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { nd_achievement_report_policy = constant:natural_disaster_report_policy.global } }
				set_country_flag = achievement_nd_global_announcer_used
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_report_policy = constant:natural_disaster_report_policy.caller } }
			var:natural_disaster_caller_country = {
				meta_effect = {
					text = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr13.[REPORT_EVENT_ID] } }
					REPORT_EVENT_ID = "[?natural_disaster_report_event_id|.0]"
				}
			}
			controller = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							var = PREV.natural_disaster_caller_country
							value = THIS
							compare = not_equals
						}
					}
					meta_effect = {
						text = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr13.[REPORT_EVENT_ID] } }
						REPORT_EVENT_ID = "[?natural_disaster_report_event_id|.0]"
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_report_policy = constant:natural_disaster_report_policy.global } }
			every_country = {
				limit = { exists = yes }
				meta_effect = {
					text = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr13.[REPORT_EVENT_ID] } }
					REPORT_EVENT_ID = "[?natural_disaster_report_event_id|.0]"
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			controller = {
				meta_effect = {
					text = { country_event = { id = chaosx.nr13.[REPORT_EVENT_ID] } }
					REPORT_EVENT_ID = "[?natural_disaster_report_event_id|.0]"
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_process_due_job = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_index = constant:natural_disaster_threshold.invalid_array_index }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_best_due_date = global.date }
	for_loop_effect = {
		start = 0
		end = natural_disaster_job_due_date_entries^num
		value = i
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = natural_disaster_job_due_date_entries^i
					value = global.date
					compare = less_than_or_equals
				}
				OR = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_job_index < 0 }
					check_variable = {
						var = natural_disaster_job_due_date_entries^i
						value = natural_disaster_job_best_due_date
						compare = less_than
					}
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_index = i }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_best_due_date = natural_disaster_job_due_date_entries^i }
		}
	}

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_job_index > constant:natural_disaster_threshold.invalid_array_index } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_type = natural_disaster_job_type_entries^natural_disaster_job_index }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_sequence_id = natural_disaster_job_sequence_id_entries^natural_disaster_job_index }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_due_date = natural_disaster_job_due_date_entries^natural_disaster_job_index }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_family = natural_disaster_job_family_entries^natural_disaster_job_index }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_followup_route = natural_disaster_job_followup_route_entries^natural_disaster_job_index }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_severity = natural_disaster_job_severity_entries^natural_disaster_job_index }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_deaths = natural_disaster_job_deaths_entries^natural_disaster_job_index }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_damage_transport = natural_disaster_job_damage_transport_entries^natural_disaster_job_index }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_damage_industry = natural_disaster_job_damage_industry_entries^natural_disaster_job_index }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_damage_ports = natural_disaster_job_damage_ports_entries^natural_disaster_job_index }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_damage_air = natural_disaster_job_damage_air_entries^natural_disaster_job_index }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_damage_resources = natural_disaster_job_damage_resources_entries^natural_disaster_job_index }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_damage_shelter = natural_disaster_job_damage_shelter_entries^natural_disaster_job_index }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_warning_choice = natural_disaster_job_warning_choice_entries^natural_disaster_job_index }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_warning_outcome = natural_disaster_job_warning_outcome_entries^natural_disaster_job_index }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_warning_matched = natural_disaster_job_warning_matched_entries^natural_disaster_job_index }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_phase = natural_disaster_job_phase_entries^natural_disaster_job_index }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_segment = natural_disaster_job_segment_entries^natural_disaster_job_index }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_chain_due_date = natural_disaster_job_chain_due_date_entries^natural_disaster_job_index }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_chain_target_state = natural_disaster_job_chain_target_state_entries^natural_disaster_job_index }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_has_chain_target = natural_disaster_job_has_chain_target_entries^natural_disaster_job_index }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_origin_family = natural_disaster_job_origin_family_entries^natural_disaster_job_index }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_origin_medium = natural_disaster_job_origin_medium_entries^natural_disaster_job_index }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_skyfall_variant = natural_disaster_job_skyfall_variant_entries^natural_disaster_job_index }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_path_basin = natural_disaster_job_path_basin_entries^natural_disaster_job_index }
		var:natural_disaster_job_target_state_entries^natural_disaster_job_index = {
			save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_impact_state
		}
		remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_target_state_entries index = natural_disaster_job_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_type_entries index = natural_disaster_job_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_sequence_id_entries index = natural_disaster_job_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_due_date_entries index = natural_disaster_job_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_family_entries index = natural_disaster_job_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_followup_route_entries index = natural_disaster_job_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_severity_entries index = natural_disaster_job_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_deaths_entries index = natural_disaster_job_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_damage_transport_entries index = natural_disaster_job_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_damage_industry_entries index = natural_disaster_job_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_damage_ports_entries index = natural_disaster_job_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_damage_air_entries index = natural_disaster_job_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_damage_resources_entries index = natural_disaster_job_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_damage_shelter_entries index = natural_disaster_job_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_warning_choice_entries index = natural_disaster_job_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_warning_outcome_entries index = natural_disaster_job_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_warning_matched_entries index = natural_disaster_job_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_phase_entries index = natural_disaster_job_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_segment_entries index = natural_disaster_job_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_chain_due_date_entries index = natural_disaster_job_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_chain_target_state_entries index = natural_disaster_job_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_has_chain_target_entries index = natural_disaster_job_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_origin_family_entries index = natural_disaster_job_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_origin_medium_entries index = natural_disaster_job_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_skyfall_variant_entries index = natural_disaster_job_index }
		remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_path_basin_entries index = natural_disaster_job_index }
		natural_disaster_release_job_day = yes

		if = {
			limit = { has_event_target = natural_disaster_impact_state }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_type = constant:natural_disaster_job_type.warning } }
				natural_disaster_execute_warning = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_type = constant:natural_disaster_job_type.impact } }
				natural_disaster_execute_impact = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_type = constant:natural_disaster_job_type.report } }
				natural_disaster_fire_family_report = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_type = constant:natural_disaster_job_type.news } }
				natural_disaster_fire_family_news = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_type = constant:natural_disaster_job_type.followup } }
				natural_disaster_execute_chain_followup = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_job_type = constant:natural_disaster_job_type.reassessment } }
				natural_disaster_execute_reassessment = yes
			}
		}
	}
	natural_disaster_achievement_mark_sequence_ended = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_job_due_date_entries^num < 1 } }
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_queue_active
	}
}

# FAMILY AND TARGET RESOLUTION

# Caller scope. Shared ordinary Evolution III pool used when the abnormal
# global cooldown blocks a random draw before any target has been promised.
natural_disaster_resolve_ordinary_family = {
	random_list = {
		10 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake } }
		10 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.flood } }
		7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tropical_cyclone } }
		8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.extreme_wind } }
		5 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tornado_outbreak } }
		8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.thunderstorm } }
		7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.hailstorm } }
		7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.blizzard } }
		7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.cold_wave } }
		7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.heat_wave } }
		7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.drought } }
		7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dust_and_sandstorm } }
		8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wildfire } }
		7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dry_mass_movement } }
		7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wet_mass_movement } }
		6 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.volcanic_eruption } }
		6 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.ashfall } }
		5 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.lahar } }
		5 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami } }
		7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.storm_surge } }
	}
}

natural_disaster_resolve_random_family = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_origin_medium = constant:natural_disaster_origin_medium.land_impact }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_skyfall_variant = constant:natural_disaster_skyfall_variant.none }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family_group > constant:natural_disaster_family_group.random }
			NOT = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_mode = constant:natural_disaster_sequence_mode.barrage } }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.seismic } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.water_coastal } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution > constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_i_stage } }
				random_list = {
					35 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.flood } }
					20 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tropical_cyclone } }
					25 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami } }
					20 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.storm_surge } }
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution > 0 } }
				random_list = {
					50 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.flood } }
					30 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tropical_cyclone } }
					20 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.storm_surge } }
				}
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.flood }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.fire_drought } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution > 0 } }
				random_list = {
					35 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.heat_wave } }
					30 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.drought } }
					35 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wildfire } }
				}
			}
			else = {
				random_list = {
					50 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.heat_wave } }
					50 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wildfire } }
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.winter_temperature } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution > 0 } }
				random_list = {
					45 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.blizzard } }
					30 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.cold_wave } }
					25 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.heat_wave } }
				}
			}
			else = {
				random_list = {
					55 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.cold_wave } }
					45 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.heat_wave } }
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.storm_weather } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution > constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_i_stage } }
				random_list = {
					15 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tropical_cyclone } }
					15 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.extreme_wind } }
					15 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tornado_outbreak } }
					20 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.thunderstorm } }
					15 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.hailstorm } }
					20 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.blizzard } }
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution > 0 } }
				random_list = {
					20 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tropical_cyclone } }
					25 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.extreme_wind } }
					20 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.thunderstorm } }
					15 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.hailstorm } }
					20 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.blizzard } }
				}
			}
			else = {
				random_list = {
					35 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.extreme_wind } }
					35 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.thunderstorm } }
					30 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.hailstorm } }
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.mass_movement } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution > 0 } }
				random_list = {
					50 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dry_mass_movement } }
					50 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wet_mass_movement } }
				}
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dry_mass_movement }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.volcanic_ash } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution > constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_i_stage } }

		}
		else_if = {

			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dust_and_sandstorm }
		}
		else = {
			random_list = {

				25 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_shower } }

	else_if = {

		}
		random_list = {
			18 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake } }
			12 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dry_mass_movement } }
			12 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wet_mass_movement } }
			15 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.volcanic_eruption } }
			10 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.lahar } }
			12 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact } }

		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_mode = constant:natural_disaster_sequence_mode.barrage }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_type = constant:natural_disaster_scenario_type.weather_crisis }
		}

			12 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.flood } }
			10 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tropical_cyclone } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.extreme_wind } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tornado_outbreak } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.thunderstorm } }
			6 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.hailstorm } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.blizzard } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.cold_wave } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.heat_wave } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.drought } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dust_and_sandstorm } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wildfire } }

	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_mode = constant:natural_disaster_sequence_mode.barrage }

			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_skyfall_variant_supplied > 0 } }

		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_intensity = constant:natural_disaster_scenario_intensity.low } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_skyfall_variant = constant:natural_disaster_skyfall_variant.local_land_impact }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_intensity = constant:natural_disaster_scenario_intensity.medium }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_intensity = constant:natural_disaster_scenario_intensity.high }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_skyfall_variant = constant:natural_disaster_skyfall_variant.severe_land_chain }
		}
		else = {
			random_list = {
				15 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_skyfall_variant = constant:natural_disaster_skyfall_variant.local_land_impact } }
				20 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_skyfall_variant = constant:natural_disaster_skyfall_variant.severe_land_chain } }
				25 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_skyfall_variant = constant:natural_disaster_skyfall_variant.meteor_shower } }
				20 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_skyfall_variant = constant:natural_disaster_skyfall_variant.skyfire_chain } }
				20 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_skyfall_variant = constant:natural_disaster_skyfall_variant.ocean_impact_chain } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_skyfall_variant = constant:natural_disaster_skyfall_variant.meteor_shower }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_skyfall_variant = constant:natural_disaster_skyfall_variant.skyfire_chain }

			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_origin_medium = constant:natural_disaster_origin_medium.land_impact }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_mode = constant:natural_disaster_sequence_mode.barrage }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_type = constant:natural_disaster_scenario_type.full_catalogue }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_intensity = constant:natural_disaster_scenario_intensity.maximum } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_ceiling = constant:natural_disaster_family.max }
			add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_ceiling = 1 }
			set_temp_variable_to_random = {
				var = natural_disaster_current_family
				min = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake
				max = natural_disaster_random_ceiling
				integer = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_ceiling = constant:natural_disaster_family.storm_surge }
			add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_ceiling = 1 }
			set_temp_variable_to_random = {
				var = natural_disaster_current_family
				min = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake
				max = natural_disaster_random_ceiling
				integer = yes
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_iii_stage }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_force_ordinary_pool > 0 }
		}
		natural_disaster_resolve_ordinary_family = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_iii_stage } }
		random_list = {
			6 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake } }
			5 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.flood } }
			4 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tropical_cyclone } }
			4 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wildfire } }
			4 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami } }
			5 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact } }
			12 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_shower } }
			10 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.whole_earth_rupture } }
			10 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.massive_eruption } }
			11 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.moving_storm_corridor } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_ii_stage } }
		random_list = {
			10 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake } }
			10 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.flood } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tropical_cyclone } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.extreme_wind } }
			5 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tornado_outbreak } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.thunderstorm } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.hailstorm } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.blizzard } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.cold_wave } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.heat_wave } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.drought } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dust_and_sandstorm } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wildfire } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dry_mass_movement } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wet_mass_movement } }
			6 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.volcanic_eruption } }
			6 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.ashfall } }
			5 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.lahar } }
			5 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.storm_surge } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_i_stage } }
		random_list = {
			12 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake } }
			12 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.flood } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tropical_cyclone } }
			10 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.extreme_wind } }
			10 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.thunderstorm } }
			9 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.hailstorm } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.blizzard } }
			9 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.cold_wave } }
			9 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.heat_wave } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.drought } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dust_and_sandstorm } }
			9 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wildfire } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dry_mass_movement } }
			8 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wet_mass_movement } }
			5 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.volcanic_eruption } }
			7 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.storm_surge } }
		}
	}
	else = {
		random_list = {
			16 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake } }
			16 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.flood } }
			11 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.extreme_wind } }
			12 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.thunderstorm } }
			10 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.hailstorm } }
			10 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.cold_wave } }
			10 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.heat_wave } }
			10 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wildfire } }
			9 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dry_mass_movement } }
		}
	}

	# Non-maximum manual barrages never draw abnormal families. Re-resolve
	# inside the selected crisis family instead of substituting a skyfall hit.
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_mode = constant:natural_disaster_sequence_mode.barrage }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_intensity < constant:natural_disaster_scenario_intensity.maximum }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family > constant:natural_disaster_family.storm_surge }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_type = constant:natural_disaster_scenario_type.geological_crisis } }
			random_list = {
				22 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake } }
				16 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dry_mass_movement } }
				16 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wet_mass_movement } }
				20 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.volcanic_eruption } }
				12 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.lahar } }
				14 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami } }
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_type = constant:natural_disaster_scenario_type.weather_crisis } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_ceiling = constant:natural_disaster_family.wildfire }
			add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_ceiling = 1 }
			set_temp_variable_to_random = {
				var = natural_disaster_current_family
				min = constant:natural_disaster_family.flood
				max = natural_disaster_random_ceiling
				integer = yes
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_type = constant:natural_disaster_scenario_type.skyfall_crisis } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact }
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_ceiling = constant:natural_disaster_family.storm_surge }
			add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_ceiling = 1 }
			set_temp_variable_to_random = {
				var = natural_disaster_current_family
				min = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake
				max = natural_disaster_random_ceiling
				integer = yes
			}
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family < constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact } }
			NOT = { natural_disaster_abnormal_family_is_allowed = yes }
			NOT = {
				AND = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_mode = constant:natural_disaster_sequence_mode.barrage }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_type = constant:natural_disaster_scenario_type.skyfall_crisis }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact }
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_force_ordinary_pool = 1 }
		natural_disaster_resolve_ordinary_family = yes
	}
}

natural_disaster_resolve_family = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family > 0 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_index > 0 } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.whole_earth_rupture } }
				random_list = {
					55 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake } }
					25 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dry_mass_movement } }
					20 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami } }
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.massive_eruption } }
				random_list = {
					45 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.ashfall } }
					30 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.lahar } }
					25 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.volcanic_eruption } }
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.moving_storm_corridor } }
				random_list = {
					35 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tornado_outbreak } }
					30 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.extreme_wind } }
					20 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.thunderstorm } }
					15 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.flood } }
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_shower } }
				random_list = {
					65 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_shower } }
					35 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact } }
				}
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami } }
				random_list = {
					65 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami } }
					35 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.storm_surge } }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family > 0 } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = natural_disaster_call_family }
	}
	else = {
		natural_disaster_resolve_random_family = yes
	}
}

natural_disaster_score_family_target_state = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_ceiling = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.random_max }
	add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_ceiling = 1 }
	set_temp_variable_to_random = {
		var = natural_disaster_target_candidate_score
		min = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.random_min
		max = natural_disaster_random_ceiling
		integer = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_dense_population = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.dense_population } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_strategic_transport = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.strategic_transport } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_industry = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.industry } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_resource_proxy = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.resources } }
		if = { limit = { is_capital = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.capital } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_previous_family = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.previous_family } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.flood } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_agricultural_proxy = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.agricultural } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_strategic_transport = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.strategic_transport } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_dense_population = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.dense_population } }
		if = { limit = { is_coastal = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.coastal } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_wet_hazard_history = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.wet_hazard } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_supply_node = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.supply_node } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tropical_cyclone } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_naval_assets = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.naval_assets } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_air_assets = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.air_assets } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_strategic_transport = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.strategic_transport } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_dense_population = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.dense_population } }
		if = { limit = { is_capital = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.capital } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_previous_family = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.previous_family } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.extreme_wind } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_air_assets = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.air_assets } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_strategic_transport = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.strategic_transport } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_industry = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.industry } }
		if = { limit = { is_coastal = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.coastal } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_previous_family = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.previous_family } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tornado_outbreak } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_agricultural_proxy = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.agricultural } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_strategic_transport = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.strategic_transport } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_dense_population = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.dense_population } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_industry = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.industry } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_air_assets = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.air_assets } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.thunderstorm } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_air_assets = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.air_assets } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_low_infrastructure = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.low_infrastructure } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_industry = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.industry } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_wet_hazard_history = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.wet_hazard } }
	}
	else_if = {

		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_agricultural_proxy = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.agricultural } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_air_assets = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.air_assets } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_industry = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.industry } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_resource_proxy = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.resources } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.blizzard } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_strategic_transport = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.strategic_transport } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_low_infrastructure = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.low_infrastructure } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_supply_node = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.supply_node } }
		if = { limit = { is_coastal = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.coastal } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_previous_family = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.previous_family } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.cold_wave } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_dense_population = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.dense_population } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_low_infrastructure = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.low_infrastructure } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_supply_node = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.supply_node } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_previous_family = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.previous_family } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.heat_wave } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_dense_population = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.dense_population } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_low_infrastructure = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.low_infrastructure } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_agricultural_proxy = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.agricultural } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_dry_hazard_history = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.dry_hazard } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_previous_family = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.previous_family } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.drought } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_agricultural_proxy = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.agricultural } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_dense_population = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.dense_population } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_resource_proxy = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.resources } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_dry_hazard_history = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.dry_hazard } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_low_infrastructure = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.low_infrastructure } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dust_and_sandstorm } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_low_infrastructure = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.low_infrastructure } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_strategic_transport = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.strategic_transport } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_air_assets = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.air_assets } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_resource_proxy = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.resources } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_dry_hazard_history = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.dry_hazard } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_war_logistics = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.war_logistics } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wildfire } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_agricultural_proxy = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.agricultural } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_resource_proxy = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.resources } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_strategic_transport = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.strategic_transport } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_dry_hazard_history = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.dry_hazard } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_previous_family = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.previous_family } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dry_mass_movement } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_strategic_transport = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.strategic_transport } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_resource_proxy = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.resources } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_low_infrastructure = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.low_infrastructure } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_dry_hazard_history = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.dry_hazard } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_previous_family = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.previous_family } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wet_mass_movement } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_strategic_transport = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.strategic_transport } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_resource_proxy = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.resources } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_low_infrastructure = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.low_infrastructure } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_wet_hazard_history = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.wet_hazard } }
