		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_previous_family = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.previous_family } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.volcanic_eruption } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_resource_proxy = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.resources } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_dense_population = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.dense_population } }
		if = { limit = { is_coastal = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.coastal } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_air_assets = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.air_assets } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_naval_assets = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.naval_assets } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_volcanic_hazard_history = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.volcanic_hazard } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.ashfall } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_air_assets = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.air_assets } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_agricultural_proxy = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.agricultural } }
		if = { limit = { is_coastal = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.coastal } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_dense_population = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.dense_population } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_industry = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.industry } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_volcanic_hazard_history = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.volcanic_hazard } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.lahar } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_strategic_transport = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.strategic_transport } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_wet_hazard_history = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.wet_hazard } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_dense_population = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.dense_population } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_volcanic_hazard_history = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.volcanic_hazard } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_resource_proxy = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.resources } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_naval_assets = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.naval_assets } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_dense_population = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.dense_population } }
		if = { limit = { is_capital = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.capital } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_strategic_transport = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.strategic_transport } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_wet_hazard_history = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.wet_hazard } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.storm_surge } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_naval_assets = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.naval_assets } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_wet_hazard_history = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.wet_hazard } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_strategic_transport = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.strategic_transport } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_dense_population = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.dense_population } }
		if = { limit = { is_coastal = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.coastal } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_dense_population = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.dense_population } }
		if = { limit = { is_capital = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.capital } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_industry = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.industry } }
		if = { limit = { is_coastal = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.coastal } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_previous_family = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.previous_family } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_shower } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_air_assets = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.air_assets } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_strategic_transport = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.strategic_transport } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_dry_hazard_history = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.dry_hazard } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_industry = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.industry } }
		if = { limit = { is_capital = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.capital } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.whole_earth_rupture } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_dense_population = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.dense_population } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_strategic_transport = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.strategic_transport } }
		if = { limit = { is_coastal = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.coastal } }
		if = { limit = { is_capital = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.capital } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_resource_proxy = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.resources } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_previous_family = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.previous_family } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.massive_eruption } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_resource_proxy = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.resources } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_dense_population = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.dense_population } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_agricultural_proxy = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.agricultural } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_air_assets = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.air_assets } }
		if = { limit = { is_coastal = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.coastal } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_volcanic_hazard_history = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.volcanic_hazard } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.moving_storm_corridor } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_strategic_transport = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.strategic_transport } }
		if = { limit = { is_coastal = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.coastal } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_air_assets = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.air_assets } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_agricultural_proxy = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.agricultural } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_dry_hazard_history = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.dry_hazard } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_dense_population = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.dense_population } }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_war_logistics = yes } add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.war_logistics } }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_active }
		add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = constant:natural_disaster_target_weight.unresolved_aftermath }
	}
}

natural_disaster_select_suitable_state_from_target_country = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_best_score = constant:natural_disaster_threshold.invalid_array_index }
	event_target:natural_disaster_target_country = {
		every_owned_state = {
			limit = {
				is_controlled_by = PREV
				natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = 0 }
			natural_disaster_score_family_target_state = yes
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score > natural_disaster_target_best_score } }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_best_score = natural_disaster_target_candidate_score }
				save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_impact_state
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_saved = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_select_suitable_state_from_target_region = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_best_score = constant:natural_disaster_threshold.invalid_array_index }
	meta_effect = {
		text = {
			every_state = {
				limit = {
					region = [REGION_ID]
					natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes
				}
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = 0 }
				natural_disaster_score_family_target_state = yes
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score > natural_disaster_target_best_score } }
					set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_best_score = natural_disaster_target_candidate_score }
					save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_impact_state
					controller = { save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_target_country }
					set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_saved = 1 }
					set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_country_candidate_saved = 1 }
				}
			}
		}
		REGION_ID = "[?natural_disaster_call_target_region|.0]"
	}
}

natural_disaster_select_state_from_target_country = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_target_country_candidate_saved > 0 }
			has_event_target = natural_disaster_target_country
			event_target:natural_disaster_target_country = {
				any_owned_state = {
					is_controlled_by = PREV
					natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes
				}
			}
		}
		natural_disaster_select_suitable_state_from_target_country = yes
	}
}

# Caller scope. Rebuilds the current target attempt's origin proof from an
# optional public-call origin or the preceding path state. The proof flag is
# reset for every attempt so an old regular event target cannot authorize a
# later ash, lahar, or tsunami.
natural_disaster_prepare_active_origin_context = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_active_origin_saved = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_active_origin_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.random }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_active_origin_medium = constant:natural_disaster_origin_medium.none }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_origin_state_supplied > 0 }
			has_event_target = natural_disaster_call_origin_state
		}
		event_target:natural_disaster_call_origin_state = { save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_active_origin_state }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_active_origin_family = natural_disaster_call_origin_family }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_active_origin_medium = natural_disaster_call_origin_medium }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_active_origin_saved = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_origin_medium > constant:natural_disaster_origin_medium.none } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_origin_medium = natural_disaster_call_origin_medium }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_index > 0 }
			has_event_target = natural_disaster_previous_path_state
		}
		event_target:natural_disaster_previous_path_state = {
			save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_active_origin_state
			if = {
				limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_family }
				ROOT = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_active_origin_family = PREV.natural_disaster_family } }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_origin_medium }
				ROOT = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_active_origin_medium = PREV.natural_disaster_origin_medium } }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_active_origin_saved = 1 }
	}
}

# Caller scope. An ocean Skyfall impact is represented by a real exposed coast
# in the chosen target domain. The coast is only an observation proxy; impact
# damage is deferred to the basin-locked tsunami subcall.
natural_disaster_select_ocean_impact_proxy = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.selected_state }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.caller_provided }
			}
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_state_supplied > 0 }
			has_event_target = natural_disaster_call_target_state
			event_target:natural_disaster_call_target_state = {
				natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes
				natural_disaster_state_is_ocean_exposed_coast = yes
			}
		}
		event_target:natural_disaster_call_target_state = {
			save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_impact_state
			controller = { save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_target_country }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_saved = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_country_candidate_saved = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.selected_country }
				AND = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.caller_provided }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_state_supplied < 1 }
				}
			}
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_country_supplied > 0 }
			has_event_target = natural_disaster_call_target_country
		}
		event_target:natural_disaster_call_target_country = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					any_owned_state = {
						is_controlled_by = PREV
						natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes
						natural_disaster_state_is_ocean_exposed_coast = yes
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_target_country
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_country_candidate_saved = 1 }
				random_owned_controlled_state = {
					limit = {
						natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes
						natural_disaster_state_is_ocean_exposed_coast = yes
					}
					save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_impact_state
					set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_saved = 1 }
				}
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.selected_region }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_region > 0 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_best_score = constant:natural_disaster_threshold.invalid_array_index }
		meta_effect = {
			text = {
				every_state = {
					limit = {
						region = [REGION_ID]
						natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes
						natural_disaster_state_is_ocean_exposed_coast = yes
					}
					set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score = 0 }
					natural_disaster_score_family_target_state = yes
					if = {
						limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_score > natural_disaster_target_best_score } }
						set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_best_score = natural_disaster_target_candidate_score }
						save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_impact_state
						controller = { save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_target_country }
						set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_saved = 1 }
						set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_country_candidate_saved = 1 }
					}
				}
			}
			REGION_ID = "[?natural_disaster_call_target_region|.0]"
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.random_valid }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.coast }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.nearby_enemy }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.dense_state }
			}
		}
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				exists = yes
				OR = {
					check_variable = { ROOT.natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.random_valid }
					check_variable = { ROOT.natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.coast }
					AND = {
						check_variable = { ROOT.natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.nearby_enemy }
						has_war_with = ROOT
					}
					check_variable = { ROOT.natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.dense_state }
				}
				any_owned_state = {
					is_controlled_by = PREV
					natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes
					natural_disaster_state_is_ocean_exposed_coast = yes
					natural_disaster_ocean_proxy_target_matches_mode = yes
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_target_country
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_country_candidate_saved = 1 }
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				limit = {
					natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes
					natural_disaster_state_is_ocean_exposed_coast = yes
					natural_disaster_ocean_proxy_target_matches_mode = yes
				}
				save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_impact_state
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_saved = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_resolve_target = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_resolved = 0 }
	# Regular event targets cannot be explicitly cleared. This proof flag keeps
	# a previous sequence hit or country from being mistaken for a newly
	# resolved target pair.
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_saved = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_country_candidate_saved = 0 }
	natural_disaster_prepare_active_origin_context = yes
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_path_target_required = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_origin_medium = constant:natural_disaster_origin_medium.ocean_impact } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_path_target_required = constant:natural_disaster_threshold.ocean_proxy_target_lock }
		natural_disaster_select_ocean_impact_proxy = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_index > 0 }
			has_event_target = natural_disaster_previous_path_state
			NOT = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.selected_state } }
			NOT = {
				AND = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.caller_provided }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_state_supplied > 0 }
				}
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.whole_earth_rupture }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.massive_eruption }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.moving_storm_corridor }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tornado_outbreak }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_path_target_required = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_origin_medium = constant:natural_disaster_origin_medium.ocean_impact } }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_path_target_required > 0 }
			event_target:natural_disaster_previous_path_state = {
				any_neighbor_state = { natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes }
			}
		}
		event_target:natural_disaster_previous_path_state = {
			random_neighbor_state = {
				limit = { natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes }
				save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_impact_state
				controller = { save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_target_country }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_saved = 1 }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_country_candidate_saved = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_path_target_required < 1 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.selected_state }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_index = 0 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_state_supplied > 0 }
			has_event_target = natural_disaster_call_target_state
			event_target:natural_disaster_call_target_state = {
				OR = {
					natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes
					natural_disaster_is_valid_repeat_impact_state = yes
				}
			}
		}
		event_target:natural_disaster_call_target_state = {
			save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_impact_state
			controller = { save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_target_country }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_saved = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_country_candidate_saved = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_path_target_required < 1 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.selected_country }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_country_supplied > 0 }
			has_event_target = natural_disaster_call_target_country
		}
		event_target:natural_disaster_call_target_country = { save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_target_country }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_country_candidate_saved = 1 }
		natural_disaster_select_state_from_target_country = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_path_target_required < 1 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.selected_region }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_region > 0 }
		}
		natural_disaster_select_suitable_state_from_target_region = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_path_target_required < 1 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.caller_provided }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_index = 0 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_state_supplied > 0 }
			has_event_target = natural_disaster_call_target_state
			event_target:natural_disaster_call_target_state = {
				OR = {
					natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes
					natural_disaster_is_valid_repeat_impact_state = yes
				}
			}
		}
		event_target:natural_disaster_call_target_state = {
			save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_impact_state
			controller = { save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_target_country }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_saved = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_country_candidate_saved = 1 }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_path_target_required < 1 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.caller_provided }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_index > 0 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_state_supplied < 1 }
			has_event_target = natural_disaster_target_country
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_country_candidate_saved = 1 }
		natural_disaster_select_state_from_target_country = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_path_target_required < 1 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.caller_provided }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_state_supplied < 1 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_country_supplied > 0 }
			has_event_target = natural_disaster_call_target_country
		}
		event_target:natural_disaster_call_target_country = { save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_target_country }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_country_candidate_saved = 1 }
		natural_disaster_select_state_from_target_country = yes
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_path_target_required < 1 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.nearby_enemy }
		}
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				exists = yes
				has_war_with = ROOT
				any_owned_state = {
					is_controlled_by = PREV
					natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes
					any_neighbor_state = {
						OR = {
							is_owned_by = ROOT
							is_controlled_by = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_target_country
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_country_candidate_saved = 1 }
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				limit = {
					natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes
					any_neighbor_state = {
						OR = {
							is_owned_by = ROOT
							is_controlled_by = ROOT
						}
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_impact_state
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_saved = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_path_target_required < 1 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.coast }
		}
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				exists = yes
				any_owned_state = {
					is_controlled_by = PREV
					natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes
					is_coastal = yes
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_target_country
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_country_candidate_saved = 1 }
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				limit = {
					natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes
					is_coastal = yes
				}
				save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_impact_state
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_saved = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_path_target_required < 1 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.dense_state }
		}
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				exists = yes
				any_owned_state = {
					is_controlled_by = PREV
					natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes
					natural_disaster_state_has_dense_population = yes
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_target_country
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_country_candidate_saved = 1 }
			random_owned_controlled_state = {
				limit = {
					natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes
					natural_disaster_state_has_dense_population = yes
				}
				save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_impact_state
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_saved = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_path_target_required < 1 }
			# A caller-pinned state must never fall through to random-country
			# resolution after its first planned hit. If the state cannot accept
			# another segment, the sequence records that segment as skipped rather
			# than widening the requested target domain.
			NOT = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.selected_state } }
			NOT = {
				AND = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.caller_provided }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_state_supplied > 0 }
				}
			}
		}
		random_country = {
			limit = {
				exists = yes
				any_owned_state = {
					is_controlled_by = PREV
					natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes
				}
			}
			save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_target_country
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_country_candidate_saved = 1 }
		}
		natural_disaster_select_state_from_target_country = yes
	}

	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_target_candidate_saved > 0 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_target_country_candidate_saved > 0 }
			has_event_target = natural_disaster_impact_state
			has_event_target = natural_disaster_target_country
			event_target:natural_disaster_impact_state = {
				OR = {
					natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes
					natural_disaster_is_valid_repeat_impact_state = yes
				}
				is_controlled_by = event_target:natural_disaster_target_country
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_resolved = 1 }
	}
}

natural_disaster_set_next_gap = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_minimum = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.baseline_min_gap_days }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_ceiling = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.baseline_max_gap_days }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_index = 0 } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_minimum = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.first_impact_min_days }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_ceiling = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.first_impact_max_days }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_iii_stage } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_minimum = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.evolution_iii_min_gap_days }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_ceiling = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.evolution_iii_max_gap_days }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_ii_stage } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_minimum = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.evolution_ii_min_gap_days }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_ceiling = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.evolution_ii_max_gap_days }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_i_stage } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_minimum = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.evolution_i_min_gap_days }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_ceiling = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.evolution_i_max_gap_days }
	}
	add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_ceiling = 1 }
	set_temp_variable_to_random = {
		var = natural_disaster_next_gap_days
		min = natural_disaster_random_minimum
		max = natural_disaster_random_ceiling
		integer = yes
	}
	add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_schedule_delay = natural_disaster_next_gap_days }
}

# State scope. Locks a resolved cyclone, tsunami, tornado, or wind corridor to
# its physical path domain. Overlapping regions use a stable priority order;
# every later segment must be adjacent and pass this same domain.
natural_disaster_capture_sequence_path_basin = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_sequence_path_family }
				NOT = {
					check_variable = {
						var = natural_disaster_sequence_path_family
						value = natural_disaster_current_family
						compare = equals
					}
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_path_basin = constant:natural_disaster_path_basin.none }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_path_family = natural_disaster_current_family }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_path_basin = constant:natural_disaster_path_basin.none }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tropical_cyclone }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.moving_storm_corridor }
			}
		}
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_is_north_atlantic_cyclone_basin = yes } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_path_basin = constant:natural_disaster_path_basin.north_atlantic } }
		else_if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_is_northeast_pacific_cyclone_basin = yes } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_path_basin = constant:natural_disaster_path_basin.northeast_pacific } }
		else_if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_is_western_north_pacific_cyclone_basin = yes } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_path_basin = constant:natural_disaster_path_basin.western_north_pacific } }
		else_if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_is_north_indian_cyclone_basin = yes } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_path_basin = constant:natural_disaster_path_basin.north_indian } }
		else_if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_is_southwest_indian_cyclone_basin = yes } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_path_basin = constant:natural_disaster_path_basin.southwest_indian } }
		else_if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_is_australian_south_pacific_cyclone_basin = yes } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_path_basin = constant:natural_disaster_path_basin.australian_south_pacific } }
		else_if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_is_tornado_zone = yes } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_path_basin = constant:natural_disaster_path_basin.tornado } }
		else = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_path_basin = constant:natural_disaster_path_basin.windstorm } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_path_basin = constant:natural_disaster_path_basin.none }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_origin_medium = constant:natural_disaster_origin_medium.ocean_impact }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_path_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami }
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_is_pacific_tsunami_basin = yes } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_path_basin = constant:natural_disaster_path_basin.pacific_tsunami } }
		else_if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_is_indian_tsunami_basin = yes } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_path_basin = constant:natural_disaster_path_basin.indian_tsunami } }
		else_if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_is_atlantic_tsunami_basin = yes } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_path_basin = constant:natural_disaster_path_basin.atlantic_tsunami } }
		else_if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_is_mediterranean_tsunami_basin = yes } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_path_basin = constant:natural_disaster_path_basin.mediterranean_tsunami } }
		else_if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_is_black_sea_tsunami_basin = yes } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_path_basin = constant:natural_disaster_path_basin.black_sea_tsunami } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_path_basin = constant:natural_disaster_path_basin.none }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami }
		}
		if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_is_pacific_tsunami_basin = yes } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_path_basin = constant:natural_disaster_path_basin.pacific_tsunami } }
		else_if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_is_indian_tsunami_basin = yes } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_path_basin = constant:natural_disaster_path_basin.indian_tsunami } }
		else_if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_is_atlantic_tsunami_basin = yes } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_path_basin = constant:natural_disaster_path_basin.atlantic_tsunami } }
		else_if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_is_mediterranean_tsunami_basin = yes } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_path_basin = constant:natural_disaster_path_basin.mediterranean_tsunami } }
		else_if = { limit = { natural_disaster_state_is_black_sea_tsunami_basin = yes } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_path_basin = constant:natural_disaster_path_basin.black_sea_tsunami } }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_path_basin = constant:natural_disaster_path_basin.none }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tornado_outbreak }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_path_basin = constant:natural_disaster_path_basin.tornado }
	}
}

natural_disaster_configure_scheduled_state = {
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_forecast_acknowledged
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_phase_partial
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_phase_failed
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_reconstruction_partial
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_rescue_success
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_stabilization_success
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_reconstruction_success
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_arrived
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_stabilize_chain_prevention_taken
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_prevented
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_partial_prevention
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_mission_success
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_mission_failed
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_terminal_failure_consequence
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_physical_chain_skipped
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_ocean_impact_proxy
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_skyfire_hail_segment
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_only_card
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_aftershock_active
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_tsunami_aftermath
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_lahar_aftermath
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_wildfire_spread
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_famine_pressure
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_disease_pressure
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_refugee_pressure
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_supply_collapse
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_active
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_arrived
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_relief_misdirected
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_relief_refused
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver.none }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_foreign_relief_status = constant:natural_disaster_relief_status.none }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_total_deaths = 0 }
	clear_variable = natural_disaster_skipped_physical_chains
	clear_variable = natural_disaster_physical_chain_sequence_id
	clear_variable = natural_disaster_chain_target_state
	clear_variable = natural_disaster_chain_family
	clear_variable = natural_disaster_chain_source_severity
	clear_variable = natural_disaster_report_due_date
	clear_variable = natural_disaster_origin_state
	clear_variable = natural_disaster_origin_family
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_segment_index = natural_disaster_sequence_index }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_segment_offset_supplied > 0 } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_segment_index = natural_disaster_call_segment_offset }
	}
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_id = global.natural_disaster_sequence_id }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_impact_index = natural_disaster_resolved_segment_index }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_family = natural_disaster_current_family }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_primary_family = natural_disaster_current_family }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_effective_origin_family = natural_disaster_planned_origin_family }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_skyfall_variant > constant:natural_disaster_skyfall_variant.none } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_effective_origin_family = natural_disaster_current_family }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_origin_state_supplied > 0 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_origin_family > constant:natural_disaster_family.random }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_effective_origin_family = natural_disaster_call_origin_family }
	}
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_origin_family = natural_disaster_effective_origin_family }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_origin_medium = natural_disaster_current_origin_medium }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_skyfall_variant = natural_disaster_current_skyfall_variant }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_skyfall_variant = constant:natural_disaster_skyfall_variant.skyfire_chain }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.hailstorm }
		}
		set_state_flag = natural_disaster_skyfire_hail_segment
	}
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_planned_hits = natural_disaster_sequence_hits }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_path_segment_index = natural_disaster_resolved_segment_index }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_path_status = constant:natural_disaster_path_status.forecast }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_path_basin = natural_disaster_sequence_path_basin }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_path_basin_family = natural_disaster_sequence_path_family }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_path_confidence = natural_disaster_warning_scale }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_path_remaining_segments = natural_disaster_sequence_hits }
	subtract_from_variable = { natural_disaster_path_remaining_segments = natural_disaster_sequence_index }
	subtract_from_variable = { natural_disaster_path_remaining_segments = 1 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = natural_disaster_previous_path_state }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_linked_state = event_target:natural_disaster_previous_path_state }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_active_origin_saved > 0 }
			has_event_target = natural_disaster_active_origin_state
		}
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_origin_state = event_target:natural_disaster_active_origin_state }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_origin_family = natural_disaster_active_origin_family }
	}
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_tsunami_origin_qualifies
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_tsunami_delayed_major_arrival
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami }
			is_coastal = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_planned_origin_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_planned_origin_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.volcanic_eruption }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_planned_origin_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_planned_origin_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.whole_earth_rupture }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_planned_origin_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.massive_eruption }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_planned_origin_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami }
			}
		}
		set_state_flag = natural_disaster_tsunami_origin_qualifies
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { nd_tsunami_coastal_groups < 1 } }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_tsunami_coastal_groups = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_tsunami_last_coast_country = OWNER }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { var = nd_tsunami_last_coast_country value = OWNER compare = not_equals }
			}
			add_to_temp_variable = { nd_tsunami_coastal_groups = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_tsunami_last_coast_country = OWNER }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = natural_disaster_sequence_index
					value = constant:natural_disaster_abnormal.delayed_tsunami_min_segment_index
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
				check_variable = {
					var = natural_disaster_call_severity
					value = constant:natural_disaster_abnormal.delayed_tsunami_min_severity
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { nd_tsunami_delayed_major_arrival = 1 }
		}
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_tsunami_coastal_groups = nd_tsunami_coastal_groups }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { nd_tsunami_delayed_major_arrival > 0 } }
			set_state_flag = natural_disaster_tsunami_delayed_major_arrival
		}
	}
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_severity = natural_disaster_call_severity }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution = natural_disaster_current_evolution }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_news_policy = natural_disaster_call_news_policy }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_report_policy = natural_disaster_call_report_policy }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_aftermath_policy = natural_disaster_call_aftermath_policy }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_policy = natural_disaster_call_chain_policy }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_death_scale = natural_disaster_call_death_scale }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_building_scale = natural_disaster_call_building_scale }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_scale = natural_disaster_call_warning_scale }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_supply_scale = natural_disaster_call_supply_scale }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_recovery_scale = natural_disaster_call_recovery_scale }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_aftermath_policy = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath_policy.light } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_recovery_scale = constant:natural_disaster_recovery_scale.light }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_aftermath_policy = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath_policy.full } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_recovery_scale = constant:natural_disaster_recovery_scale.full }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_aftermath_policy = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath_policy.emergency } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_recovery_scale = constant:natural_disaster_recovery_scale.emergency }
	}
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_recovery_scale = natural_disaster_resolved_recovery_scale }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_caller_type = natural_disaster_call_caller_type }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_caller_event_id = natural_disaster_call_caller_event_id }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_caller_country = ROOT }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_schedule_date = global.date }
	add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_schedule_date = natural_disaster_schedule_delay }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.warning }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.warning }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_preparation_score = 0 }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_score = 0 }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_score = 0 }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_reconstruction_score = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_recovery_burden = natural_disaster_resolved_recovery_scale }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_recovery_burden = 1.00 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_recovery_burden = 2.00 }
	round_temp_variable = natural_disaster_recovery_burden
	multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_recovery_burden = -1 }
	add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_score = natural_disaster_recovery_burden }
	add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_score = natural_disaster_recovery_burden }
	add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_reconstruction_score = natural_disaster_recovery_burden }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution > 1 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_score = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.evolution_ii_score_penalty }
		subtract_from_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_score = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.evolution_ii_score_penalty }
		subtract_from_variable = { natural_disaster_reconstruction_score = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.evolution_ii_score_penalty }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution > 2 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_score = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.evolution_iii_score_penalty }
		subtract_from_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_score = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.evolution_iii_score_penalty }
		subtract_from_variable = { natural_disaster_reconstruction_score = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.evolution_iii_score_penalty }
	}
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_cleanup_state = 0 }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.none }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.none }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_prevention_score = 0 }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_outcome = 0 }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_choice = constant:natural_disaster_warning_choice.none }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_protection = constant:natural_disaster_warning_protection.none }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_action_result = constant:natural_disaster_warning_choice.none }
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_evolution_ii_card_sync_applied
	set_state_flag = natural_disaster_impact_scheduled
	set_state_flag = {
		flag = natural_disaster_impact_guard
		days = @ND_IMPACT_GUARD_DAYS
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_index = 0 } }
		OWNER = { save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_log_actor }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_sequence_id = natural_disaster_sequence_id }
	OWNER = { natural_disaster_achievement_begin_sequence = yes }
	natural_disaster_register_abnormal_gui_card = yes
	natural_disaster_set_category_display_from_state = yes
	controller = {
		set_country_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_category_visible
		set_country_flag = natural_disaster_warning_unread
	}
}

natural_disaster_configure_repeated_impact = {
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_forecast_acknowledged
	clear_variable = natural_disaster_origin_state
	clear_variable = natural_disaster_origin_family
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_active_origin_saved > 0 }
			has_event_target = natural_disaster_active_origin_state
		}
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_origin_state = event_target:natural_disaster_active_origin_state }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_origin_family = natural_disaster_active_origin_family }
	}
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_path_basin = natural_disaster_sequence_path_basin }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_path_basin_family = natural_disaster_sequence_path_family }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_sequence_id = global.natural_disaster_sequence_id }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_segment_index = natural_disaster_sequence_index }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_segment_offset_supplied > 0 } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_segment_index = natural_disaster_call_segment_offset }
	}
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_impact_index = natural_disaster_resolved_segment_index }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_family = natural_disaster_current_family }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_effective_origin_family = natural_disaster_planned_origin_family }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_skyfall_variant > constant:natural_disaster_skyfall_variant.none } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_effective_origin_family = natural_disaster_current_family }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_origin_state_supplied > 0 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_origin_family > constant:natural_disaster_family.random }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_effective_origin_family = natural_disaster_call_origin_family }
	}
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_origin_family = natural_disaster_effective_origin_family }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_origin_medium = natural_disaster_current_origin_medium }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_skyfall_variant = natural_disaster_current_skyfall_variant }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_planned_hits = natural_disaster_sequence_hits }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_severity = natural_disaster_call_severity }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_evolution = natural_disaster_current_evolution }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_news_policy = natural_disaster_call_news_policy }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_report_policy = natural_disaster_call_report_policy }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_aftermath_policy = natural_disaster_call_aftermath_policy }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_chain_policy = natural_disaster_call_chain_policy }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_death_scale = natural_disaster_call_death_scale }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_building_scale = natural_disaster_call_building_scale }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_recovery_scale = natural_disaster_call_recovery_scale }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_supply_scale = natural_disaster_call_supply_scale }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_caller_country = ROOT }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_schedule_date = global.date }
	add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_schedule_date = natural_disaster_schedule_delay }
	set_state_flag = natural_disaster_repeat_impact_scheduled
	set_state_flag = natural_disaster_impact_scheduled
	set_state_flag = { flag = natural_disaster_impact_guard days = @ND_IMPACT_GUARD_DAYS }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_sequence_id = natural_disaster_repeat_sequence_id }
	OWNER = { natural_disaster_achievement_begin_sequence = yes }
}

natural_disaster_schedule_warning_and_impact = {
	event_target:natural_disaster_impact_state = {
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_scheduled = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_open_card = yes }
			natural_disaster_configure_repeated_impact = yes
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_scheduled = 1 }
		}
		else = {
			natural_disaster_configure_scheduled_state = yes
		}

		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_percent = constant:natural_disaster_warning.base_chance }
		if = {
			limit = { infrastructure > constant:natural_disaster_threshold.strategic_infrastructure }
			add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_percent = constant:natural_disaster_warning.good_infrastructure_bonus }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { radar_station > 0 }
			add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_percent = constant:natural_disaster_warning.radar_bonus }
		}
		OWNER = {
			if = {
				limit = { has_war = yes }
				add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_percent = constant:natural_disaster_warning.war_penalty }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { has_stability < constant:natural_disaster_factor.low_stability_threshold }
				add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_percent = constant:natural_disaster_warning.low_stability_penalty }
			}
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_percent = natural_disaster_call_warning_scale }
		clamp_temp_variable = {
			var = natural_disaster_warning_percent
			min = constant:natural_disaster_warning.minimum_chance
			max = constant:natural_disaster_warning.maximum_chance
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_percent = constant:natural_disaster_factor.percent_to_random_chance }
		round_temp_variable = natural_disaster_warning_percent
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_index = 0 }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_type = constant:natural_disaster_caller.random }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_type = constant:natural_disaster_caller.cluster }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_percent = constant:natural_disaster_factor.percent_to_random_chance }
		}

		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_delay = natural_disaster_schedule_delay }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_delay = 1 }
		clamp_temp_variable = {
			var = natural_disaster_warning_delay
			min = constant:natural_disaster_threshold.minimum_job_delay_days
			max = natural_disaster_schedule_delay
		}

		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_scheduled < 1 } }
			random = {
				chance = natural_disaster_warning_percent
				set_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_scheduled
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_type = constant:natural_disaster_job_type.warning }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_delay_days = natural_disaster_warning_delay }
				natural_disaster_enqueue_state_job = yes
			}
		}

		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_type = constant:natural_disaster_job_type.impact }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_delay_days = natural_disaster_schedule_delay }
		natural_disaster_enqueue_state_job = yes
	}
}

natural_disaster_plan_sequence = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_index = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_schedule_delay = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_scheduled_hits = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_skipped_hits = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.random }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_resolved = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_path_target_required = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_preselected_family = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_force_ordinary_pool = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_contains_abnormal = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_path_basin = constant:natural_disaster_path_basin.none }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_path_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.random }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_active_origin_saved = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_active_origin_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.random }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_tsunami_coastal_groups = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_tsunami_last_coast_country = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_tsunami_delayed_major_arrival = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_iii_stage }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.random }
			NOT = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_mode = constant:natural_disaster_sequence_mode.barrage } }
			natural_disaster_abnormal_family_is_allowed = yes
		}
		random_list = {
			10 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_preselected_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact } }
			20 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_preselected_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_shower } }
			20 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_preselected_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.whole_earth_rupture } }
			20 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_preselected_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.massive_eruption } }
			20 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_preselected_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.moving_storm_corridor } }
			10 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_preselected_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tornado_outbreak } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family > 0 }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.abnormal }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_mode = constant:natural_disaster_sequence_mode.moving_corridor }
				NOT = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family < constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact } }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family = natural_disaster_call_family }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_planned_origin_family = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_origin_state_supplied > 0 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_origin_family > constant:natural_disaster_family.random }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_planned_origin_family = natural_disaster_call_origin_family }
	}

	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_index < natural_disaster_sequence_hits } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_origin_medium = constant:natural_disaster_origin_medium.land_impact }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_skyfall_variant = constant:natural_disaster_skyfall_variant.none }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = constant:natural_disaster_event.evolution_iii_stage }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.random }
				NOT = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family < constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact } }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_scheduled_hits = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.evolution_iii_abnormal_path_hits }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_force_ordinary_pool = 1 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_index = 0 }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_preselected_family > 0 }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family < 1 }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = natural_disaster_preselected_family }
		}
		else = {
			natural_disaster_resolve_family = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_shower }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_skyfall_variant = constant:natural_disaster_skyfall_variant.none }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_origin_medium = constant:natural_disaster_origin_medium.atmospheric_fragmentation }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_causal_context_supplied > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_origin_medium = natural_disaster_call_causal_origin_medium }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_skyfall_variant = natural_disaster_call_causal_skyfall_variant }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_resolved = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_path_target_required = 0 }
		natural_disaster_resolve_target = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_target_resolved < 1 }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.random }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_path_target_required < 1 }
					AND = {
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_mode = constant:natural_disaster_sequence_mode.barrage }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_type = constant:natural_disaster_scenario_type.skyfall_crisis }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_skyfall_variant_supplied < 1 }
					}
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_target_attempt = 1 }
			while_loop_effect = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_target_resolved < 1 }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_random_target_attempt < constant:natural_disaster_sequence.random_target_attempts }
				}
				natural_disaster_resolve_family = yes
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_target_resolved = 0 }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_path_target_required = 0 }
				natural_disaster_resolve_target = yes
				add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_target_attempt = 1 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_target_resolved < 1 }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.random }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_path_target_required < 1 }
					AND = {
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_mode = constant:natural_disaster_sequence_mode.barrage }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_type = constant:natural_disaster_scenario_type.skyfall_crisis }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_skyfall_variant_supplied < 1 }
					}
				}
			}
			natural_disaster_exhaust_random_family_targets = yes
		}
		natural_disaster_set_next_gap = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_target_resolved > 0 } }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family < constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact } } }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_contains_abnormal = 1 }
			}
			event_target:natural_disaster_impact_state = { natural_disaster_capture_sequence_path_basin = yes }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_planned_origin_family < 1 } }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_planned_origin_family = natural_disaster_current_family }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_resolved_primary_family = natural_disaster_current_family }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_has_resolved_primary_state = 1 }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_has_resolved_primary_country = 1 }
				event_target:natural_disaster_impact_state = { save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_call_resolved_primary_state }
				event_target:natural_disaster_target_country = { save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_call_resolved_primary_country }
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.selected_region } }
					set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_resolved_target_region = natural_disaster_call_target_region }
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family < 1 }
						NOT = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family < constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact } }
						NOT = {
							AND = {
								check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_mode = constant:natural_disaster_sequence_mode.barrage }
								check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_type = constant:natural_disaster_scenario_type.skyfall_crisis }
							}
						}
					}
					set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_locked_family = natural_disaster_current_family }
				}
			}
			natural_disaster_schedule_warning_and_impact = yes
			event_target:natural_disaster_impact_state = { save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_previous_path_state }
			add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_scheduled_hits = 1 }
		}
		else = {
			add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_skipped_hits = 1 }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_index = 1 }
	}

}

natural_disaster_call = {
	# Seed every public output and optional input in this outer controller before
	# nested scripted effects modify it. Temporary variables first created in a
	# nested scripted effect are not guaranteed to survive that effect's return.
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_result = constant:natural_disaster_call_result.rejected }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_reject_reason = constant:natural_disaster_call_reject_reason.none }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_id = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_primary_job_count = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_resolved_primary_family = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_has_resolved_primary_state = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_has_resolved_primary_country = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_resolved_target_region = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_skipped_primary_count = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = constant:natural_disaster_event.baseline_stage }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution_i_enabled = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution_ii_enabled = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution_iii_enabled = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.random }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_valid = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_hits = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_scheduled_hits = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_skipped_hits = 0 }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_caller_type } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_type = constant:natural_disaster_caller.random } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_caller_event_id } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_event_id = constant:natural_disaster_event.id } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_family } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.random } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_family_group } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.random } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_target_mode } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.random_valid } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_target_region } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_region = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_origin_state_supplied } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_origin_state_supplied = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_origin_family } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_origin_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.random } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_origin_medium } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_origin_medium = constant:natural_disaster_origin_medium.none } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_evolution_override_supplied } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_evolution_override_supplied = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_evolution_override } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_evolution_override = constant:natural_disaster_event.baseline_stage } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_severity } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.local } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_sequence_mode } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_mode = constant:natural_disaster_sequence_mode.local_season } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_sequence_count } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_count = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_news_policy } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_news_policy = constant:natural_disaster_news_policy.meaningful } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_report_policy } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_report_policy = constant:natural_disaster_report_policy.affected_country } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_aftermath_policy } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_aftermath_policy = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath_policy.normal } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_chain_policy } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_chain_policy = constant:natural_disaster_chain_policy.family } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_death_scale } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_death_scale = 1.00 } }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_building_scale } }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_damage_scale }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_building_scale = natural_disaster_call_damage_scale }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_building_scale = 1.00 } }
	}
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_warning_scale } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_warning_scale = 1.00 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_recovery_scale } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_recovery_scale = 1.00 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_supply_scale } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_supply_scale = 1.00 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_caller_cost_checked } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_cost_checked = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_caller_cooldown_checked } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_cooldown_checked = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_target_legitimacy_checked } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_legitimacy_checked = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_log_mode } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_log_mode = constant:natural_disaster_log_mode.event_013_history } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_scenario_type } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_type = constant:natural_disaster_scenario_type.random_barrage } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_scenario_intensity } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_scenario_intensity = constant:natural_disaster_scenario_intensity.low } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_skyfall_variant_supplied } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_skyfall_variant_supplied = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_skyfall_variant } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_skyfall_variant = constant:natural_disaster_skyfall_variant.none } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_causal_context_supplied } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_causal_context_supplied = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_causal_origin_medium } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_causal_origin_medium = constant:natural_disaster_origin_medium.none } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_causal_skyfall_variant } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_causal_skyfall_variant = constant:natural_disaster_skyfall_variant.none } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_sequence_id_override_supplied } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_id_override_supplied = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_sequence_id_override } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_id_override = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_segment_offset_supplied } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_segment_offset_supplied = 0 } }
