	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_segment_offset } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_segment_offset = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_internal_chain_override } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_internal_chain_override = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_manual_evolution_bypass } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_manual_evolution_bypass = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_manual_abnormal_bypass } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_manual_abnormal_bypass = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_target_state_supplied } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_state_supplied = 0 } }
	if = { limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_call_target_country_supplied } } set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_country_supplied = 0 } }
	natural_disaster_set_call_defaults = yes
	natural_disaster_refresh_evolution_state = yes
	natural_disaster_validate_call = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_valid > 0 } }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_evolution_override_supplied > 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = natural_disaster_call_evolution_override }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family > constant:natural_disaster_family.random } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = natural_disaster_call_family }
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { natural_disaster_current_family_available_for_evolution = yes } }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_valid = 0 }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_reject_reason = constant:natural_disaster_call_reject_reason.invalid_evolution_override }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_valid > 0 } }
		natural_disaster_resolve_call_severity = yes
		natural_disaster_resolve_sequence_count = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = global.natural_disaster_sequence_id }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_id_existed = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_previous_sequence_id = global.natural_disaster_sequence_id }
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_id_existed = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_previous_sequence_id = 0 }
			set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_sequence_id = 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_id_override_supplied > 0 } }
			set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_sequence_id = natural_disaster_call_sequence_id_override }
		}
		else = {
			add_to_variable = { global.natural_disaster_sequence_id = 1 }
		}
		natural_disaster_plan_sequence = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_scheduled_hits > 0 } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_id_override_supplied < 1 } }
				set_variable = { natural_disaster_last_sequence_id = global.natural_disaster_sequence_id }
				set_variable = { natural_disaster_last_sequence_planned_hits = natural_disaster_sequence_hits }
				set_variable = { natural_disaster_last_sequence_scheduled_hits = natural_disaster_scheduled_hits }
				set_variable = { natural_disaster_last_sequence_skipped_hits = natural_disaster_skipped_hits }
				set_country_flag = natural_disaster_sequence_anchor
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_result = constant:natural_disaster_call_result.accepted }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_id = global.natural_disaster_sequence_id }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_primary_job_count = natural_disaster_scheduled_hits }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_skipped_primary_count = natural_disaster_skipped_hits }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_contains_abnormal > 0 }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_manual_abnormal_bypass < 1 }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_internal_chain_override < 1 }
					NOT = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_type = constant:natural_disaster_caller.scenario } }
					NOT = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_type = constant:natural_disaster_caller.debug } }
				}
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_abnormal_cooldown_days = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.abnormal_global_cooldown_days }
				set_global_flag = {
					flag = natural_disaster_abnormal_global_cooldown
					days = natural_disaster_abnormal_cooldown_days
				}
			}
			natural_disaster_record_reached_evolutions = yes
			natural_disaster_record_call_history = yes
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.heat_wave }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_state_supplied > 0 }
					has_event_target = natural_disaster_call_target_state
					event_target:natural_disaster_call_target_state = {
						OWNER = {
							OR = {
								has_idea = heat_wave
								has_country_flag = natural_disaster_heat_exclusion
							}
						}
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_reject_reason = constant:natural_disaster_call_reject_reason.heat_exclusion }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family > constant:natural_disaster_family.random }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_state_supplied > 0 }
					OR = {
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.selected_state }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.caller_provided }
					}
				}
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_reject_reason = constant:natural_disaster_call_reject_reason.invalid_target }
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_reject_reason = constant:natural_disaster_call_reject_reason.no_eligible_target }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_id_existed > 0 } }
				set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_sequence_id = natural_disaster_previous_sequence_id }
			}
			else = {
				clear_variable = global.natural_disaster_sequence_id
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_id_override_supplied > 0 } }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_id_existed > 0 } }
				set_variable = { global.natural_disaster_sequence_id = natural_disaster_previous_sequence_id }
			}
			else = { clear_variable = global.natural_disaster_sequence_id }
		}
		}
	}
}

# ============================================================================
# FAMILY PROFILES AND IMPACT RESOLUTION
# ============================================================================

natural_disaster_apply_resilience_profile = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_death_mult = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_building_mult = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_disruption_mult = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_related = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_partial_resilience_related = 0 }

	if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = natural_disaster_resilient_rails
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_transport > 0 }
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_building_mult = constant:natural_disaster_resilience.general_transport_damage_mult }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_disruption_mult = constant:natural_disaster_resilience.general_transport_damage_mult }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_death_mult = constant:natural_disaster_resilience.secondary_death_mult }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = natural_disaster_resilient_rails_partial
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_transport > 0 }
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_building_mult = constant:natural_disaster_resilience.partial_transport_damage_mult }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_disruption_mult = constant:natural_disaster_resilience.partial_transport_damage_mult }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = natural_disaster_seismic_retrofit
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.whole_earth_rupture }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_related = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = natural_disaster_coastal_barriers
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.flood }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tropical_cyclone }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.storm_surge }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_related = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = natural_disaster_firebreak_network
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wildfire }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_related = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = natural_disaster_volcanic_exclusion_routes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.volcanic_eruption }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.ashfall }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.lahar }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.massive_eruption }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_related = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = natural_disaster_water_security
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.heat_wave }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.drought }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dust_and_sandstorm }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_related = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = natural_disaster_exclusion_cordon
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_shower }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_related = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = natural_disaster_weather_shelter_network
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.extreme_wind }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tornado_outbreak }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.thunderstorm }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.hailstorm }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.blizzard }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.cold_wave }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.moving_storm_corridor }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_related = 1 }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = natural_disaster_slope_stabilization
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dry_mass_movement }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wet_mass_movement }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_related = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_related < 1 }
			OR = {
				AND = {
					has_state_flag = natural_disaster_seismic_retrofit_partial
					OR = {
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.whole_earth_rupture }
					}
				}
				AND = {
					has_state_flag = natural_disaster_coastal_barriers_partial
					OR = {
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.flood }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tropical_cyclone }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.storm_surge }
					}
				}
				AND = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_firebreak_network_partial check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wildfire } }
				AND = {
					has_state_flag = natural_disaster_volcanic_exclusion_routes_partial
					OR = {
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.volcanic_eruption }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.ashfall }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.lahar }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.massive_eruption }
					}
				}
				AND = {
					has_state_flag = natural_disaster_water_security_partial
					OR = {
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.heat_wave }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.drought }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dust_and_sandstorm }
					}
				}
				AND = {
					has_state_flag = natural_disaster_exclusion_cordon_partial
					OR = {
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_shower }
					}
				}
				AND = {
					has_state_flag = natural_disaster_weather_shelter_network_partial
					OR = {
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.extreme_wind }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tornado_outbreak }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.thunderstorm }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.hailstorm }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.blizzard }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.cold_wave }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.moving_storm_corridor }
					}
				}
				AND = {
					has_state_flag = natural_disaster_slope_stabilization_partial
					OR = {
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dry_mass_movement }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wet_mass_movement }
					}
				}
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_partial_resilience_related = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_related > 0 } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_death_mult = constant:natural_disaster_resilience.related_death_mult }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_building_mult = constant:natural_disaster_resilience.related_building_mult }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_disruption_mult = constant:natural_disaster_resilience.related_disruption_mult }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_partial_resilience_related > 0 } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_death_mult = constant:natural_disaster_resilience.partial_related_death_mult }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_building_mult = constant:natural_disaster_resilience.partial_related_building_mult }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_disruption_mult = constant:natural_disaster_resilience.partial_related_disruption_mult }
	}
}

		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.abnormal }

	if = {
		limit = {

		}
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_choice = constant:natural_disaster_warning_choice.primary }

	if = {

			check_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_choice = constant:natural_disaster_warning_choice.primary }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_protection = constant:natural_disaster_warning_protection.none }

		set_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_protection = natural_disaster_warning_primary_protection }
	}
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = natural_disaster_resolved_family_group }
}

	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_tertiary_building = token:arms_factory }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_default_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.aftershock }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_transport = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_industry = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_ports = 0 }

	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_news_event_id = constant:natural_disaster_event.news_event_base }
	add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_news_event_id = natural_disaster_current_family }
	natural_disaster_apply_resilience_profile = yes
}

natural_disaster_prepare_severity_profile = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.abnormal } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_fraction = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.abnormal }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_points = constant:natural_disaster_damage.abnormal_points }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_modifier_days = constant:natural_disaster_damage.abnormal_duration_days }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_supply = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.abnormal_supply }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_supply_impact = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.abnormal_supply_impact }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_speed = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.abnormal_speed }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_attrition = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.abnormal_attrition }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_repair = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.abnormal_repair }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_resources = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.abnormal_resources }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.catastrophic } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_fraction = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.catastrophic }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_points = constant:natural_disaster_damage.catastrophic_points }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_modifier_days = constant:natural_disaster_damage.catastrophic_duration_days }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_supply = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.catastrophic_supply }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_supply_impact = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.catastrophic_supply_impact }

		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_speed = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.regional_speed }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_attrition = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.regional_attrition }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_repair = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.regional_repair }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_resources = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.regional_resources }

	else_if = {

		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_fraction = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.severe }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_points = constant:natural_disaster_damage.severe_points }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_modifier_days = constant:natural_disaster_damage.severe_duration_days }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_supply = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.severe_supply }
