natural_disaster_damage_selected_building = {
	meta_effect = {
		text = {
			damage_building = {
				type = [BUILDING_TYPE]
				damage = [BUILDING_DAMAGE]
			}
		}
		BUILDING_TYPE = "[?natural_disaster_damage_building.GetTokenKey]"
		BUILDING_DAMAGE = "[?natural_disaster_building_damage|2]"
	}
}

natural_disaster_apply_family_building_damage = {
	set_temp_variable = { nd_resolved_building_scale = natural_disaster_building_scale }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = nd_effect_building_scale
			check_variable = { nd_effect_building_scale > 0 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { nd_resolved_building_scale = nd_effect_building_scale }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_outcome > 0 } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { nd_resolved_building_scale = constant:natural_disaster_factor.warning_building_damage }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_preparation_score > 0 } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { nd_resolved_building_scale = constant:natural_disaster_factor.prepared_building_damage }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_protection = constant:natural_disaster_warning_protection.transport }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_protection = constant:natural_disaster_warning_protection.industry_air }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_protection = constant:natural_disaster_warning_protection.coast }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_protection = constant:natural_disaster_warning_protection.fire }
			}
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = { nd_resolved_building_scale = constant:natural_disaster_warning.material_protection_mult }
	}
	multiply_temp_variable = { nd_resolved_building_scale = natural_disaster_resilience_building_mult }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_building_damage = natural_disaster_damage_points }
	multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_building_damage = nd_resolved_building_scale }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_building = natural_disaster_primary_building }
	natural_disaster_damage_selected_building = yes

	multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_building_damage = constant:natural_disaster_factor.secondary_building_damage }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_building = natural_disaster_secondary_building }
	natural_disaster_damage_selected_building = yes

	multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_building_damage = constant:natural_disaster_factor.tertiary_from_secondary_damage }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_building = natural_disaster_tertiary_building }
	natural_disaster_damage_selected_building = yes

	set_temp_variable = { nd_card_damage_points = natural_disaster_damage_points }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_card_damage_transport = natural_disaster_damage_transport }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_card_damage_industry = natural_disaster_damage_industry }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_card_damage_ports = natural_disaster_damage_ports }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_card_damage_air = natural_disaster_damage_air }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_card_damage_resources = natural_disaster_damage_resources }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_card_damage_shelter = natural_disaster_damage_shelter }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_points = nd_card_damage_points }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_transport = nd_card_damage_transport }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_industry = nd_card_damage_industry }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_ports = nd_card_damage_ports }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_air = nd_card_damage_air }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_resources = nd_card_damage_resources }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_shelter = nd_card_damage_shelter }
}

natural_disaster_apply_death_driver_candidate = {
	multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_multiplier = natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate_factor }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate_priority
				value = natural_disaster_selected_death_driver_priority
				compare = greater_than
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_selected_death_driver_priority = natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate_priority }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_death_driver = natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate }
	}
}

# State scope. Builds a capped casualty-vulnerability multiplier from physical
# exposure and strategic conditions, while retaining the strongest observed
# cause as a stable card and abnormal-history summary.
natural_disaster_prepare_death_driver_profile = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_multiplier = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_selected_death_driver_priority = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_death_driver = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver.none }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.seismic } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_death_driver = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver.seismic_exposure }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.water_coastal } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_death_driver = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver.water_coastal_exposure }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.storm_weather } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_death_driver = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver.storm_weather_exposure }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.winter_temperature } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_death_driver = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver.winter_temperature_exposure }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.fire_drought } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_death_driver = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver.fire_drought_exposure }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.mass_movement } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_death_driver = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver.mass_movement_exposure }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.volcanic_ash } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_death_driver = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver.volcanic_ash_exposure }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.dust_sand } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_death_driver = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver.dust_sand_exposure }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.abnormal } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_death_driver = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver.abnormal_exposure }
	}

	if = {
		limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_dense_population = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver.dense_population }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate_factor = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver_factor.dense_population }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate_priority = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver_priority.dense_population }
		natural_disaster_apply_death_driver_candidate = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_low_infrastructure = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver.low_infrastructure }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate_factor = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver_factor.low_infrastructure }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate_priority = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver_priority.low_infrastructure }
		natural_disaster_apply_death_driver_candidate = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			natural_disaster_state_is_ocean_exposed_coast = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tropical_cyclone }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.storm_surge }
				AND = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_origin_medium = constant:natural_disaster_origin_medium.ocean_impact }
				}
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.whole_earth_rupture }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver.coastal_population }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate_factor = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver_factor.coastal_population }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate_priority = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver_priority.coastal_population }
		natural_disaster_apply_death_driver_candidate = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			natural_disaster_state_has_strategic_transport = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.seismic }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.water_coastal }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.winter_temperature }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.mass_movement }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.dust_sand }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.abnormal }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver.strategic_transport }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate_factor = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver_factor.strategic_transport }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate_priority = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver_priority.strategic_transport }
		natural_disaster_apply_death_driver_candidate = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			natural_disaster_state_has_air_assets = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.storm_weather }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.ashfall }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_shower }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.moving_storm_corridor }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver.air_assets }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate_factor = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver_factor.air_assets }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate_priority = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver_priority.air_assets }
		natural_disaster_apply_death_driver_candidate = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			natural_disaster_state_has_agricultural_proxy = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.water_coastal }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.storm_weather }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.winter_temperature }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.fire_drought }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.volcanic_ash }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.dust_sand }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver.agricultural_exposure }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate_factor = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver_factor.agricultural_exposure }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate_priority = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver_priority.agricultural_exposure }
		natural_disaster_apply_death_driver_candidate = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				natural_disaster_state_is_wildfire_fuel_zone = yes
				natural_disaster_state_is_arid_zone = yes
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.heat_wave }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.drought }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dust_and_sandstorm }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wildfire }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dry_mass_movement }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver.dry_fuel }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate_factor = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver_factor.dry_fuel }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate_priority = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver_priority.dry_fuel }
		natural_disaster_apply_death_driver_candidate = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			natural_disaster_state_is_slope_zone = yes
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dry_mass_movement }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wet_mass_movement }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.lahar }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.whole_earth_rupture }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver.slope_valley }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate_factor = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver_factor.slope_valley }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate_priority = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver_priority.slope_valley }
		natural_disaster_apply_death_driver_candidate = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				natural_disaster_state_is_volcanic_vent_zone = yes
				natural_disaster_state_is_volcanic_plume_zone = yes
				natural_disaster_state_is_lahar_zone = yes
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.volcanic_eruption }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.ashfall }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.lahar }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.massive_eruption }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver.volcanic_proximity }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate_factor = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver_factor.volcanic_proximity }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate_priority = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver_priority.volcanic_proximity }
		natural_disaster_apply_death_driver_candidate = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OWNER = { has_war = yes }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.winter_temperature }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.fire_drought }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.dust_sand }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.abnormal }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver.war_logistics }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate_factor = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver_factor.war_logistics }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate_priority = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver_priority.war_logistics }
		natural_disaster_apply_death_driver_candidate = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_active }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver.unresolved_devastation }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate_factor = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver_factor.unresolved_devastation }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate_priority = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver_priority.unresolved_devastation }
		natural_disaster_apply_death_driver_candidate = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_outcome < 1 }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tornado_outbreak }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.thunderstorm }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.hailstorm }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wildfire }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dry_mass_movement }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wet_mass_movement }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.volcanic_eruption }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.lahar }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.whole_earth_rupture }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver.warning_failure }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate_factor = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver_factor.warning_failure }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate_priority = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver_priority.warning_failure }
		natural_disaster_apply_death_driver_candidate = yes
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.abnormal }
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_path_segment_index > 1 }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_total_deaths > 0 }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver.repeated_abnormal_impacts }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate_factor = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver_factor.repeated_abnormal_impacts }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate_priority = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver_priority.repeated_abnormal_impacts }
		natural_disaster_apply_death_driver_candidate = yes
	}
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = natural_disaster_death_driver_multiplier
		min = 1
		max = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver_factor.maximum_combined
	}
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver = natural_disaster_resolved_death_driver }
}

natural_disaster_apply_population_loss = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_multiplier = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_selected_death_driver_priority = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_death_driver = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver.none }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver.none }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate_factor = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver_candidate_priority = 0 }
	natural_disaster_prepare_death_driver_profile = yes
	set_temp_variable = { nd_resolved_death_scale = natural_disaster_death_scale }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = nd_effect_death_scale
			check_variable = { nd_effect_death_scale > 0 }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { nd_resolved_death_scale = nd_effect_death_scale }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_multiplier = natural_disaster_family_lethality }
	multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_multiplier = nd_resolved_death_scale }
	multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_multiplier = natural_disaster_resilience_death_mult }
	multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_multiplier = natural_disaster_death_driver_multiplier }

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution > 1 } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_multiplier = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.evolution_ii_mult }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution > 2 } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_multiplier = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.evolution_iii_mult }
	}
	OWNER = {
		if = {
			limit = { has_war = yes }
			multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_multiplier = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.war_mult }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_stability < constant:natural_disaster_factor.low_stability_threshold }
			multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_multiplier = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.low_stability_mult }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_active }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_multiplier = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.unresolved_aftermath_mult }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_outcome > 0 } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_multiplier = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.warning_success_mult }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_preparation_score > 0 } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_multiplier = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.prepared_mult }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_protection = constant:natural_disaster_warning_protection.population }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_protection = constant:natural_disaster_warning_protection.food_water }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_protection = constant:natural_disaster_warning_protection.coast }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_protection = constant:natural_disaster_warning_protection.medical }
			}
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_multiplier = constant:natural_disaster_warning.population_protection_mult }
	}

	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_cap_factor = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.local_cap_factor }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.severe } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_cap_factor = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.severe_cap_factor }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.regional } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_cap_factor = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.regional_cap_factor }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.catastrophic } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_cap_factor = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.catastrophic_cap_factor }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.abnormal } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_cap_factor = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.abnormal_cap_factor }
	}

	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_population_cap = state_population_k }
	multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_population_cap = constant:natural_disaster_factor.population_thousands_to_people }
	multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_population_cap = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.maximum_state_fraction }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_cap_floor = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.minimum_people }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_death_cap_floor > natural_disaster_population_cap } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_cap_floor = natural_disaster_population_cap }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { chaos_deaths_change = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { chaos_state_deaths_percent = natural_disaster_death_fraction }
	set_temp_variable = { chaos_state_deaths_mult = natural_disaster_death_multiplier }
	set_temp_variable = { chaos_state_deaths_cap_factor = natural_disaster_death_cap_factor }
	set_temp_variable = { chaos_state_deaths_cap_floor = natural_disaster_death_cap_floor }
	set_temp_variable = { chaos_state_deaths_cap_hard_max = natural_disaster_population_cap }
	set_temp_variable = { chaos_state_deaths_exact_percent = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { chaos_deaths_reason = constant:chaos_meter_deaths_reason.natural_disaster }
	chaos_meter_register_state_civilian_deaths_percent = yes
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_last_deaths = chaos_deaths_change }
	add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_total_deaths = chaos_deaths_change }
}

# State scope after population loss. Event 012's hostile nature action persists
# its caller type, caller ID, and caller country on the impacted state. Record
# the civilian-weaponisation disqualifier only after this exact Event 013
# impact has produced positive civilian deaths. Generic Event 013 calls,
# warnings, building damage, and target selection alone cannot open the flag.
natural_disaster_record_event012_civilian_weaponisation = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = natural_disaster_caller_type
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_caller_type = constant:natural_disaster_caller.hostile_actor }
			has_variable = natural_disaster_caller_event_id
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_caller_event_id = constant:africa_natural_disaster.caller_event_id }
			has_variable = natural_disaster_caller_country
			has_variable = natural_disaster_last_deaths
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_last_deaths > 0 }
			NOT = { has_global_flag = africa_achievement_disaster_weaponised_against_civilians }
		}
		var:natural_disaster_caller_country = {
			africa_achievement_record_disaster_weaponised_against_civilians = yes
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_clear_family_disruption_modifiers = {
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = natural_disaster_seismic_disruption }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = natural_disaster_water_coastal_disruption }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = natural_disaster_storm_weather_disruption }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = natural_disaster_winter_temperature_disruption }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = natural_disaster_fire_drought_disruption }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = natural_disaster_mass_movement_disruption }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = natural_disaster_volcanic_ash_disruption }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = natural_disaster_dust_sand_disruption }
	remove_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = natural_disaster_abnormal_disruption }
}

natural_disaster_apply_family_disruption_modifier = {
	natural_disaster_clear_family_disruption_modifiers = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family > constant:natural_disaster_family.storm_surge } }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = natural_disaster_abnormal_disruption }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.seismic } }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = natural_disaster_seismic_disruption }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.water_coastal } }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = natural_disaster_water_coastal_disruption }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.storm_weather } }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = natural_disaster_storm_weather_disruption }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.winter_temperature } }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = natural_disaster_winter_temperature_disruption }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.fire_drought } }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = natural_disaster_fire_drought_disruption }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.mass_movement } }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = natural_disaster_mass_movement_disruption }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.volcanic_ash } }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = natural_disaster_volcanic_ash_disruption }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.dust_sand } }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = natural_disaster_dust_sand_disruption }
	}
	else = {
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = natural_disaster_abnormal_disruption }
	}
}

natural_disaster_apply_state_disruption = {
	set_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_supply = natural_disaster_mod_supply }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_supply_impact = natural_disaster_mod_supply_impact }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_speed = natural_disaster_mod_speed }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_attrition = natural_disaster_mod_attrition }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_repair = natural_disaster_mod_repair }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_resources = natural_disaster_mod_resources }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_transport < 1 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_ports < 1 }
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_supply = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.incidental_system_factor }
		multiply_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_supply_impact = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.incidental_system_factor }
		multiply_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_speed = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.secondary_system_factor }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_shelter < 1 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_air < 1 }
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_attrition = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.secondary_system_factor }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_shelter < 1 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_air > 0 }
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_attrition = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.airfield_attrition_factor }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_transport < 1 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_industry < 1 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_ports < 1 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_air < 1 }
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_repair = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.incidental_system_factor }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_resources < 1 } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_resources = constant:natural_disaster_modifier.incidental_system_factor }
	}
	multiply_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_supply = natural_disaster_supply_scale }
	multiply_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_supply_impact = natural_disaster_supply_scale }
	multiply_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_supply = natural_disaster_resilience_disruption_mult }
	multiply_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_supply_impact = natural_disaster_resilience_disruption_mult }
	multiply_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_speed = natural_disaster_resilience_disruption_mult }
	multiply_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_attrition = natural_disaster_resilience_disruption_mult }
	multiply_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_repair = natural_disaster_resilience_disruption_mult }
	multiply_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_resources = natural_disaster_resilience_disruption_mult }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_state_mod_days = natural_disaster_modifier_days }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_supply = nd_state_mod_supply }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_supply_impact = nd_state_mod_supply_impact }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_speed = nd_state_mod_speed }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_attrition = nd_state_mod_attrition }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_repair = nd_state_mod_repair }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_resources = nd_state_mod_resources }
	natural_disaster_apply_family_disruption_modifier = yes
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_modifier_expiry = global.date }
	add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_modifier_expiry = nd_state_mod_days }
}

# State scope. Chooses the exact causal route listed by the expanded family
# mini-spec. The warning decision never supplies or changes this value.
natural_disaster_resolve_family_followup_route = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake } }
		random_list = {
		1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.aftershock } }
		1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.seismic_landslide } }
		1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.urban_fire } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.flood } }
		random_list = {
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.waterborne_disease } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.refugee_pressure } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.crop_loss } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.dam_failure_flash_flood } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tropical_cyclone } }
		random_list = {
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.storm_surge } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.inland_flood } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.disease } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.supply_collapse } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.extreme_wind } }
		random_list = {
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.wildfire_spread } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.rail_derailment } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.transport_collapse } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tornado_outbreak } }
		random_list = {
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.severe_thunderstorm_tail } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.wildfire_line } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.refugee_pressure } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.thunderstorm } }
		random_list = {
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.hailstorm } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.flash_flood } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.wildfire_ignition } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.hailstorm } }
		random_list = {
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.ration_pressure } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.airfield_accident } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.thunderstorm_return } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.blizzard } }
		random_list = {
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.extreme_cold_wave } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.supply_collapse } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.refugee_exposure } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.cold_wave } }
		random_list = {
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.blizzard_followup } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.shelter_disease } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.supply_collapse } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.heat_wave } }
		random_list = {
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.wildfire } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.drought } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.unrest } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.drought } }
		random_list = {
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.famine_pressure } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.wildfire } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.refugee_pressure } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dust_and_sandstorm } }
		random_list = {
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.drought } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.disease } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.heat_wave } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wildfire } }
		random_list = {
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.smoke_illness } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.refugee_pressure } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.drought_feedback } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dry_mass_movement } }
		random_list = {
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.supply_isolation } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.aftershock_damage } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.resource_shutdown } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wet_mass_movement } }
		random_list = {
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.flood_renewal } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.disease } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.supply_isolation } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.volcanic_eruption } }
		random_list = {
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.ashfall } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.lahar } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.famine_pressure } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.ashfall } }
		random_list = {
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.respiratory_deaths } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.famine_pressure } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.lahar } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.lahar } }
		random_list = {
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.flood } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.disease } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.supply_isolation } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami } }
		random_list = {
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.disease } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.refugee_pressure } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.naval_disruption } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.coastal_famine } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.storm_surge } }
		random_list = {
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.flood } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.disease } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.port_supply_collapse } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact } }
		if = {
			limit = { is_coastal = yes }
			random_list = {
				2 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.wildfire } }
				1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.dust_veil } }
				2 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.tsunami } }
				1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.meteor_shower } }
			}
		}
		else = {
			random_list = {
				2 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.wildfire } }
				1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.dust_veil } }
				1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.meteor_shower } }
			}
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_shower } }
		random_list = {
			2 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.wildfire } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.transport_collapse } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.meteor_impact } }
			2 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.hailstorm } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.whole_earth_rupture } }
		random_list = {
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.global_aftershocks } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.delayed_tsunami_chain } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.regional_landslides } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.urban_fire } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.massive_eruption } }
		random_list = {
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.regional_ashfall } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.lahar } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.tsunami } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.famine_pressure } }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.moving_storm_corridor } }
		random_list = {
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.tornado_outbreak } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.flood } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.wildfire } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.storm_surge } }
		}
	}
}

# State scope. Converts a fine route into the compact mechanical executor used
# by missions, outcome scaling, achievements, and socioeconomic consequences.
natural_disaster_map_followup_route_to_chain = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.none }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.aftershock }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.seismic_landslide }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.aftershock_damage }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.global_aftershocks }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.regional_landslides }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.aftershock }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.offshore_tsunami }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.tsunami }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.delayed_tsunami_chain }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.tsunami }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.crop_loss }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.ration_pressure }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.famine_pressure }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.coastal_famine }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.famine }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.waterborne_disease }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.disease }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.shelter_disease }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.smoke_illness }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.respiratory_deaths }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.disease }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.urban_fire }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.wildfire_spread }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.wildfire_line }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.wildfire_ignition }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.wildfire }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.wildfire_spread }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.refugee_pressure }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.refugee_exposure }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.refugee_pressure }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.lahar } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.lahar }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.unrest } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.political_shock }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route > constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.none } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.supply_collapse }
	}
}

natural_disaster_resolve_chain_risk = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.none }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.none }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_policy = constant:natural_disaster_chain_policy.famine } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.famine_pressure }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_policy = constant:natural_disaster_chain_policy.disease } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.disease }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_policy = constant:natural_disaster_chain_policy.refugee } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.refugee_pressure }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_policy = constant:natural_disaster_chain_policy.infrastructure } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.supply_collapse }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_policy = constant:natural_disaster_chain_policy.tsunami } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.tsunami }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_policy = constant:natural_disaster_chain_policy.aftershock } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.aftershock }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_policy = constant:natural_disaster_chain_policy.family }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_policy = constant:natural_disaster_chain_policy.abnormal_path }
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity > constant:natural_disaster_severity.local }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution > 1 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = natural_disaster_current_severity
					value = constant:natural_disaster_severity.regional
					compare = greater_than_or_equals
				}
				is_coastal = yes
				OR = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.volcanic_eruption }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_geological_tsunami_chance = constant:natural_disaster_chain_weight.geological_tsunami }
			random = {
				chance = natural_disaster_geological_tsunami_chance
				if = {
					limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake } }
					set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.offshore_tsunami }
				}
				else = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.tsunami } }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.none } }
			natural_disaster_resolve_family_followup_route = yes
		}
	}
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = natural_disaster_resolved_route }
	natural_disaster_map_followup_route_to_chain = yes
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = natural_disaster_resolved_chain }
}

# State scope. Abnormal Skyfall variants own their causal route. Ordinary
# impacts continue through the family resolver; the route remains independently
# resolved from warning preparation.
natural_disaster_resolve_causal_chain_risk = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.none }
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_skyfall_variant = constant:natural_disaster_skyfall_variant.skyfire_chain }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_origin_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_shower }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_shower }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.skyfire_hail }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = natural_disaster_resolved_route }
		natural_disaster_map_followup_route_to_chain = yes
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = natural_disaster_resolved_chain }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_skyfall_variant = constant:natural_disaster_skyfall_variant.severe_land_chain }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact }
			check_variable = {
				var = natural_disaster_current_severity
				value = constant:natural_disaster_severity.catastrophic
				compare = greater_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		random_list = {
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.wildfire } }
			1 = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.dust_veil } }
		}
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = natural_disaster_resolved_route }
		natural_disaster_map_followup_route_to_chain = yes
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = natural_disaster_resolved_chain }
	}
	else = { natural_disaster_resolve_chain_risk = yes }
}

natural_disaster_prepare_warning_profile_costs = {
	set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_manpower_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost.local_manpower }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_support_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost.local_support }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_truck_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost.local_trucks }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_train_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost.local_trains }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_convoy_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost.local_convoys }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_fuel_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost.local_fuel }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_command_power_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost.local_command_power }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_stability_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost_fixed.local_stability }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_war_support_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost_fixed.local_war_support }
	if = {
		limit = { FROM = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.severe } } }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_manpower_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost.severe_manpower }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_support_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost.severe_support }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_truck_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost.severe_trucks }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_train_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost.severe_trains }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_convoy_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost.severe_convoys }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_fuel_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost.severe_fuel }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_command_power_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost.severe_command_power }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_stability_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost_fixed.severe_stability }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_war_support_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost_fixed.severe_war_support }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { FROM = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.regional } } }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_manpower_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost.regional_manpower }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_support_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost.regional_support }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_truck_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost.regional_trucks }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_train_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost.regional_trains }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_convoy_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost.regional_convoys }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_fuel_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost.regional_fuel }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_command_power_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost.regional_command_power }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_stability_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost_fixed.regional_stability }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_war_support_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost_fixed.regional_war_support }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { FROM = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.catastrophic } } }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_manpower_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost.catastrophic_manpower }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_support_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost.catastrophic_support }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_truck_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost.catastrophic_trucks }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_train_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost.catastrophic_trains }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_convoy_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost.catastrophic_convoys }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_fuel_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost.catastrophic_fuel }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_command_power_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost.catastrophic_command_power }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_stability_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost_fixed.catastrophic_stability }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_war_support_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost_fixed.catastrophic_war_support }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { FROM = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.abnormal } } }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_manpower_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost.abnormal_manpower }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_support_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost.abnormal_support }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_truck_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost.abnormal_trucks }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_train_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost.abnormal_trains }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_convoy_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost.abnormal_convoys }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_fuel_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost.abnormal_fuel }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_command_power_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost.abnormal_command_power }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_stability_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost_fixed.abnormal_stability }
		set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_war_support_cost = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost_fixed.abnormal_war_support }
	}
}

natural_disaster_deduct_warning_manpower = {
	set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_payment = nd_warning_manpower_cost }
	multiply_temp_variable = { nd_warning_payment = -1 }
	add_manpower = nd_warning_payment
}

	set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_payment = nd_warning_support_cost }
	multiply_temp_variable = { nd_warning_payment = -1 }
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = nd_warning_payment }
}

natural_disaster_add_warning_civilian_burden = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_variable = natural_disaster_warning_civilian_burden_count } }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_civilian_burden_count = 0 }
	}
	add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_civilian_burden_count = 1 }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_civilian_burden_value = natural_disaster_warning_civilian_burden_count }
	multiply_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_civilian_burden_value = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost_fixed.civilian_burden_per_action }
	set_country_flag = natural_disaster_warning_civilian_burden_active
	remove_dynamic_modifier = natural_disaster_warning_civilian_burden
	add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = natural_disaster_warning_civilian_burden }
	country_event = { id = chaosx.nr13.6 days = @ND_WARNING_ACTION_LOCK_DAYS }
}

natural_disaster_release_warning_civilian_burden = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_civilian_burden_count > 0 } }
		subtract_from_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_civilian_burden_count = 1 }
	}
	remove_dynamic_modifier = natural_disaster_warning_civilian_burden
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_civilian_burden_count > 0 } }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_civilian_burden_value = natural_disaster_warning_civilian_burden_count }
		multiply_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_civilian_burden_value = constant:natural_disaster_warning_cost_fixed.civilian_burden_per_action }
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = natural_disaster_warning_civilian_burden }
	}
	else = {
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_warning_civilian_burden_active
		clear_variable = natural_disaster_warning_civilian_burden_count
		clear_variable = natural_disaster_warning_civilian_burden_value
	}
}

	set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_stability_cost = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_warning_war_support_cost = 0 }

	}
}

natural_disaster_apply_warning_direction = {
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_choice = nd_warning_choice }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_protection = nd_warning_protection }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_action_result = nd_warning_choice }
	add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_preparation_score = constant:natural_disaster_warning.preparation_score }
	set_state_flag = { flag = natural_disaster_warning_action_taken days = @ND_WARNING_ACTION_LOCK_DAYS }
}

natural_disaster_refresh_aftermath_capacity = {
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.base_rescue_cap }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.base_stabilization_cap }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_reconstruction_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.base_reconstruction_cap }
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_major = no
			num_of_factories < constant:natural_disaster_threshold.weak_country_factory_count
		}
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.weak_country_cap }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.weak_country_cap }
		subtract_from_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.minimum_active_cap }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_reconstruction_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.weak_country_reconstruction_cap }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { is_major = yes }
		add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.major_country_bonus_cap }
		add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.major_country_bonus_cap }
		add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_reconstruction_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.major_country_bonus_cap }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_country_flag = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_capacity_bonus }
		add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.foreign_relief_bonus_cap }
		add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.foreign_relief_bonus_cap }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_war = yes }
		subtract_from_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.war_cap_penalty }
		subtract_from_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.war_cap_penalty }
		subtract_from_variable = { natural_disaster_reconstruction_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.war_cap_penalty }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_controlled_state = {
				natural_disaster_state_has_recovery_card = yes
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution > 1 }
			}
		}
		add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.evolution_pressure_bonus_cap }
		add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.evolution_pressure_bonus_cap }
		add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_reconstruction_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.evolution_pressure_bonus_cap }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_maximum_started
			NOT = { has_country_flag = achievement_nd_barrage_maximum_ended }
		}
		add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.barrage_bonus_cap }
		add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.barrage_bonus_cap }
		add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_reconstruction_cap = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.barrage_bonus_cap }
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = natural_disaster_rescue_cap
		min = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.minimum_active_cap
		max = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.maximum_rescue_cap
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = natural_disaster_stabilization_cap
		min = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.minimum_active_cap
		max = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.maximum_stabilization_cap
	}
	clamp_variable = {
		var = natural_disaster_reconstruction_cap
		min = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.minimum_active_cap
		max = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.maximum_reconstruction_cap
	}
}

natural_disaster_activate_rescue_mission_for_current_state = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_candidate_state = PREV }
	natural_disaster_refresh_aftermath_capacity = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_cap > 0 }
			NOT = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_1 }
		}
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_state_1 = natural_disaster_candidate_state }
		activate_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_1
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_cap > 1 }
			NOT = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_2 }
		}
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_state_2 = natural_disaster_candidate_state }
		activate_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_2
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_cap > 2 }
			NOT = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_3 }
		}
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_state_3 = natural_disaster_candidate_state }
		activate_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_3
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_cap > 3 }
			NOT = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_4 }
		}
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_state_4 = natural_disaster_candidate_state }
		activate_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_4
	}
}

natural_disaster_activate_stabilization_mission_for_current_state = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_candidate_state = PREV }
	natural_disaster_refresh_aftermath_capacity = yes
	if = {

		}
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_state_2 = natural_disaster_candidate_state }
		activate_mission = natural_disaster_stabilization_mission_2
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_cap > 2 }
			NOT = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_stabilization_mission_3 }
		}
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_state_3 = natural_disaster_candidate_state }
		activate_mission = natural_disaster_stabilization_mission_3
	}
}

natural_disaster_activate_reconstruction_mission_for_current_state = {

	natural_disaster_refresh_aftermath_capacity = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_reconstruction_cap > 0 }
