		activate_mission = natural_disaster_reconstruction_mission_2
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_reconstruction_cap > 2 }
			NOT = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_reconstruction_mission_3 }
		}
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_reconstruction_state_3 = natural_disaster_candidate_state }
		activate_mission = natural_disaster_reconstruction_mission_3
	}
}

# Country scope. Selects the most urgent unslotted severe card in the requested
# phase. Input: natural_disaster_priority_phase.
natural_disaster_select_severe_reservation_card = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_severe_reservation_selected = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_best_score = -1 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_variant = 0 }
	every_controlled_state = {
		limit = {
			natural_disaster_state_has_recovery_card = yes
			NOT = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_recovery_unresolved_territory }
			natural_disaster_state_matches_severe_reservation_phase = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_score = 0 }
		natural_disaster_score_priority_state = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_score > natural_disaster_priority_best_score } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_best_score = natural_disaster_priority_score }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_severe_reservation_selected = 1 }
			save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_severe_reservation_state
		}
	}
}

# Country scope. If rescue capacity is saturated by ordinary cards, move one
# lower-severity slot to the selected severe card. Highest-numbered slots are
# displaced first so the minimum-cap slot remains stable whenever possible.
natural_disaster_preempt_rescue_slot_for_severe_card = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			event_target:natural_disaster_severe_reservation_state = { NOT = { natural_disaster_state_has_rescue_slot = yes } }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_cap > 3 }
			has_active_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_4
			has_variable = natural_disaster_rescue_state_4
			var:natural_disaster_rescue_state_4 = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_severity < constant:natural_disaster_severity.severe } }
		}
		remove_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_4
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_state_4 = event_target:natural_disaster_severe_reservation_state }
		activate_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_4
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			event_target:natural_disaster_severe_reservation_state = { NOT = { natural_disaster_state_has_rescue_slot = yes } }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_cap > 2 }
			has_active_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_3
			has_variable = natural_disaster_rescue_state_3
			var:natural_disaster_rescue_state_3 = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_severity < constant:natural_disaster_severity.severe } }
		}
		remove_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_3
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_state_3 = event_target:natural_disaster_severe_reservation_state }
		activate_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_3
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			event_target:natural_disaster_severe_reservation_state = { NOT = { natural_disaster_state_has_rescue_slot = yes } }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_cap > 1 }
			has_active_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_2
			has_variable = natural_disaster_rescue_state_2
			var:natural_disaster_rescue_state_2 = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_severity < constant:natural_disaster_severity.severe } }
		}
		remove_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_2
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_state_2 = event_target:natural_disaster_severe_reservation_state }
		activate_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_2
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			event_target:natural_disaster_severe_reservation_state = { NOT = { natural_disaster_state_has_rescue_slot = yes } }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_cap > 0 }
			has_active_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_1
			has_variable = natural_disaster_rescue_state_1
			var:natural_disaster_rescue_state_1 = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_severity < constant:natural_disaster_severity.severe } }
		}
		remove_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_1
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_state_1 = event_target:natural_disaster_severe_reservation_state }
		activate_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_1
	}
}

natural_disaster_preempt_stabilization_slot_for_severe_card = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			event_target:natural_disaster_severe_reservation_state = { NOT = { natural_disaster_state_has_stabilization_slot = yes } }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_cap > 2 }
			has_active_mission = natural_disaster_stabilization_mission_3
			has_variable = natural_disaster_stabilization_state_3
			var:natural_disaster_stabilization_state_3 = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_severity < constant:natural_disaster_severity.severe } }
		}
		remove_mission = natural_disaster_stabilization_mission_3
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_state_3 = event_target:natural_disaster_severe_reservation_state }
		activate_mission = natural_disaster_stabilization_mission_3
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			event_target:natural_disaster_severe_reservation_state = { NOT = { natural_disaster_state_has_stabilization_slot = yes } }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_cap > 1 }
			has_active_mission = natural_disaster_stabilization_mission_2
			has_variable = natural_disaster_stabilization_state_2
			var:natural_disaster_stabilization_state_2 = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_severity < constant:natural_disaster_severity.severe } }
		}
		remove_mission = natural_disaster_stabilization_mission_2
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_state_2 = event_target:natural_disaster_severe_reservation_state }
		activate_mission = natural_disaster_stabilization_mission_2
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			event_target:natural_disaster_severe_reservation_state = { NOT = { natural_disaster_state_has_stabilization_slot = yes } }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_cap > 0 }
			has_active_mission = natural_disaster_stabilization_mission_1
			has_variable = natural_disaster_stabilization_state_1
			var:natural_disaster_stabilization_state_1 = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_severity < constant:natural_disaster_severity.severe } }
		}
		remove_mission = natural_disaster_stabilization_mission_1
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_state_1 = event_target:natural_disaster_severe_reservation_state }
		activate_mission = natural_disaster_stabilization_mission_1
	}
}

natural_disaster_preempt_reconstruction_slot_for_severe_card = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			event_target:natural_disaster_severe_reservation_state = { NOT = { natural_disaster_state_has_reconstruction_slot = yes } }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_reconstruction_cap > 2 }
			has_active_mission = natural_disaster_reconstruction_mission_3
			has_variable = natural_disaster_reconstruction_state_3
			var:natural_disaster_reconstruction_state_3 = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_severity < constant:natural_disaster_severity.severe } }
		}
		remove_mission = natural_disaster_reconstruction_mission_3
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_reconstruction_state_3 = event_target:natural_disaster_severe_reservation_state }
		activate_mission = natural_disaster_reconstruction_mission_3
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			event_target:natural_disaster_severe_reservation_state = { NOT = { natural_disaster_state_has_reconstruction_slot = yes } }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_reconstruction_cap > 1 }
			has_active_mission = natural_disaster_reconstruction_mission_2
			has_variable = natural_disaster_reconstruction_state_2
			var:natural_disaster_reconstruction_state_2 = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_severity < constant:natural_disaster_severity.severe } }
		}
		remove_mission = natural_disaster_reconstruction_mission_2
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_reconstruction_state_2 = event_target:natural_disaster_severe_reservation_state }
		activate_mission = natural_disaster_reconstruction_mission_2
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			event_target:natural_disaster_severe_reservation_state = { NOT = { natural_disaster_state_has_reconstruction_slot = yes } }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_reconstruction_cap > 0 }
			has_active_mission = natural_disaster_reconstruction_mission_1
			has_variable = natural_disaster_reconstruction_state_1
			var:natural_disaster_reconstruction_state_1 = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_severity < constant:natural_disaster_severity.severe } }
		}
		remove_mission = natural_disaster_reconstruction_mission_1
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_reconstruction_state_1 = event_target:natural_disaster_severe_reservation_state }
		activate_mission = natural_disaster_reconstruction_mission_1
	}
}

# Country scope. Guarantees one phase slot to the most urgent severe card while
# leaving every other slot under the normal dynamic priority system.
natural_disaster_ensure_severe_phase_reservations = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.early_rescue }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { natural_disaster_country_has_severe_reserved_slot_for_phase = yes } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_severe_reservation_selected = 0 }
		natural_disaster_select_severe_reservation_card = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_severe_reservation_selected > 0 } }
			event_target:natural_disaster_severe_reservation_state = {
				controller = { natural_disaster_activate_rescue_mission_for_current_state = yes }
			}
			natural_disaster_preempt_rescue_slot_for_severe_card = yes
		}
	}
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.middle_stabilization }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { natural_disaster_country_has_severe_reserved_slot_for_phase = yes } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_severe_reservation_selected = 0 }
		natural_disaster_select_severe_reservation_card = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_severe_reservation_selected > 0 } }
			event_target:natural_disaster_severe_reservation_state = {
				controller = { natural_disaster_activate_stabilization_mission_for_current_state = yes }
			}
			natural_disaster_preempt_stabilization_slot_for_severe_card = yes
		}
	}
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.late_reconstruction }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { natural_disaster_country_has_severe_reserved_slot_for_phase = yes } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_severe_reservation_selected = 0 }
		natural_disaster_select_severe_reservation_card = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_severe_reservation_selected > 0 } }
			event_target:natural_disaster_severe_reservation_state = {
				controller = { natural_disaster_activate_reconstruction_mission_for_current_state = yes }
			}
			natural_disaster_preempt_reconstruction_slot_for_severe_card = yes
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_fill_open_aftermath_capacity = {
	natural_disaster_refresh_aftermath_capacity = yes
	natural_disaster_ensure_severe_phase_reservations = yes
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_capacity_iteration = 0 }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_capacity_iteration < constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.maximum_rescue_cap } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.early_rescue }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_selected = 0 }
		natural_disaster_select_priority_open_card = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_selected > 0 } }
				event_target:natural_disaster_priority_open_card_state = {
					controller = { natural_disaster_activate_rescue_mission_for_current_state = yes }
			}
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_capacity_iteration = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.maximum_rescue_cap } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_capacity_iteration = 1 }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_capacity_iteration = 0 }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_capacity_iteration < constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.maximum_stabilization_cap } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.middle_stabilization }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_selected = 0 }
		natural_disaster_select_priority_open_card = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_selected > 0 } }
				event_target:natural_disaster_priority_open_card_state = {
					controller = { natural_disaster_activate_stabilization_mission_for_current_state = yes }
			}
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_capacity_iteration = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.maximum_stabilization_cap } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_capacity_iteration = 1 }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_capacity_iteration = 0 }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_capacity_iteration < constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.maximum_reconstruction_cap } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.late_reconstruction }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_selected = 0 }
		natural_disaster_select_priority_open_card = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_selected > 0 } }
				event_target:natural_disaster_priority_open_card_state = {
					controller = { natural_disaster_activate_reconstruction_mission_for_current_state = yes }
			}
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_capacity_iteration = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.maximum_reconstruction_cap } }
		add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_capacity_iteration = 1 }
	}
}

natural_disaster_activate_family_chain_mission = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = natural_disaster_chain_mission_active }
			PREV = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.tsunami }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.disease }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.famine }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.wildfire_spread }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.supply_collapse }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.lahar }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.aftershock }
				}
			}
		}
		set_country_flag = natural_disaster_chain_mission_active
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_mission_state = PREV }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension = PREV.natural_disaster_chain_due_date }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension = global.date }
		subtract_from_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_mission_deadline_offset_days }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension < 0 } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension = 0 }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.tsunami } } }
			activate_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_tsunami_mission
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension > 0 } }
				meta_effect = {
					text = { add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_tsunami_mission days = [DAYS] } }
					DAYS = "[?natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension|.0]"
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.disease } } }
			activate_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_disease_mission
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension > 0 } }
				meta_effect = {
					text = { add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_disease_mission days = [DAYS] } }
					DAYS = "[?natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension|.0]"
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.famine } } }
			activate_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_famine_mission
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension > 0 } }
				meta_effect = {
					text = { add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_famine_mission days = [DAYS] } }
					DAYS = "[?natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension|.0]"
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.wildfire_spread } } }
			activate_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_wildfire_spread_mission
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension > 0 } }
				meta_effect = {
					text = { add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_wildfire_spread_mission days = [DAYS] } }
					DAYS = "[?natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension|.0]"
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.supply_collapse } } }
			activate_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_supply_collapse_mission
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension > 0 } }
				meta_effect = {
					text = { add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_supply_collapse_mission days = [DAYS] } }
					DAYS = "[?natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension|.0]"
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.lahar } } }
			activate_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_lahar_mission
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension > 0 } }
				meta_effect = {
					text = { add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_lahar_mission days = [DAYS] } }
					DAYS = "[?natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension|.0]"
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			activate_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_aftershock_mission
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension > 0 } }
				meta_effect = {
					text = { add_days_mission_timeout = { mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_aftershock_mission days = [DAYS] } }
					DAYS = "[?natural_disaster_chain_mission_extension|.0]"
				}
			}
		}
	}
}

# Country scope. The single typed chain slot always refills from the earliest
# unresolved due date, so later cards cannot starve an older warning. The scan
# is bounded to this country's controlled states and runs only on card changes.
natural_disaster_select_next_chain_mission_state = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_refill_selected = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_refill_best_due = constant:natural_disaster_priority.chain_due_sentinel }
	every_controlled_state = {
		limit = {
			natural_disaster_state_has_unresolved_chain = yes
			NOT = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_recovery_unresolved_territory }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_due_date > 0 }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_due_date < natural_disaster_chain_refill_best_due }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_refill_best_due = natural_disaster_chain_due_date }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_refill_selected = 1 }
		save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_chain_refill_state
	}
}

natural_disaster_fill_chain_mission_capacity = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = natural_disaster_chain_mission_active } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_refill_selected = 0 }
		natural_disaster_select_next_chain_mission_state = yes
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_refill_selected > 0 }
				has_event_target = natural_disaster_chain_refill_state
			}
			event_target:natural_disaster_chain_refill_state = {
				controller = { natural_disaster_activate_family_chain_mission = yes }
			}
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_schedule_capacity_refill = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_country_flag = natural_disaster_capacity_refill_pending } }
		set_country_flag = natural_disaster_capacity_refill_pending
		country_event = { id = chaosx.nr13.7 days = 1 }
	}
}

# Country scope. Registers only countries that currently control at least one
# actionable recovery card. Calls are limited to card and control-change paths.
natural_disaster_register_relief_recipient_country = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			any_controlled_state = {
				natural_disaster_state_has_recovery_card = yes
				NOT = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_recovery_unresolved_territory }
			}
			NOT = {
				is_in_array = {
					array = global.natural_disaster_relief_recipient_countries
					value = THIS
				}
			}
		}
		add_to_array = {
			array = global.natural_disaster_relief_recipient_countries
			value = THIS
		}
	}
}

# Country scope. Removes the country only after its last actionable recovery
# card is gone; other open cards keep the country registered.
natural_disaster_unregister_relief_recipient_country_if_inactive = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			is_in_array = {
				array = global.natural_disaster_relief_recipient_countries
				value = THIS
			}
			NOT = {
				any_controlled_state = {
					natural_disaster_state_has_recovery_card = yes
					NOT = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_recovery_unresolved_territory }
				}
			}
		}
		remove_from_array = {
			array = global.natural_disaster_relief_recipient_countries
			value = THIS
		}
	}
}

# State scope. Persist the actual physical route selected for inbound aid so
# the recipient's route decision and displayed cost stay deterministic.
natural_disaster_set_inbound_relief_route_from_state = {
	controller = {
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_route_convoy
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_route_rail
		if = {
			limit = { PREV = { natural_disaster_state_uses_convoy_recovery_route = yes } }
			set_country_flag = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_route_convoy
		}
		else = { set_country_flag = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_route_rail }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_relief_status_value = constant:natural_disaster_relief_status.pledged }
	natural_disaster_set_foreign_relief_status = yes
}

# State scope. Input: pre-seeded temporary variable
# natural_disaster_relief_status_value. The abnormal-history updater safely
# ignores ordinary cards without a registered history row.
natural_disaster_set_foreign_relief_status = {
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_foreign_relief_status = natural_disaster_relief_status_value }
	natural_disaster_update_abnormal_history_record = yes
}

natural_disaster_begin_outbound_relief_light_burden = {
	set_country_flag = natural_disaster_outbound_relief_active
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_relief_burden_days = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.foreign_relief_days }
	add_timed_idea = {
		idea = natural_disaster_outbound_relief_light_burden
		days = natural_disaster_relief_burden_days
	}
}

natural_disaster_begin_outbound_relief_heavy_burden = {
	set_country_flag = natural_disaster_outbound_relief_active
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_relief_burden_days = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.foreign_relief_days }
	add_timed_idea = {
		idea = natural_disaster_outbound_relief_burden
		days = natural_disaster_relief_burden_days
	}
}

natural_disaster_pay_neighbor_relief = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_relief_manpower_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.rescue_manpower }
	multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_relief_manpower_cost = -1 }
	add_manpower = natural_disaster_relief_manpower_cost
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_relief_support_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.foreign_support_equipment }
	multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_relief_support_cost = -1 }
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = natural_disaster_relief_support_cost }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_relief_truck_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.foreign_trucks }
	multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_relief_truck_cost = -1 }
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = natural_disaster_relief_truck_cost }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_relief_convoy_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.foreign_convoys }
	multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_relief_convoy_cost = -1 }
	add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = convoy_1 amount = natural_disaster_relief_convoy_cost }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_relief_fuel_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.foreign_fuel }
	multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_relief_fuel_cost = -1 }
	add_fuel = natural_disaster_relief_fuel_cost
	add_stability = constant:natural_disaster_cost_fixed.foreign_relief_stability
	add_war_support = constant:natural_disaster_cost_fixed.foreign_relief_war_support
	natural_disaster_begin_outbound_relief_light_burden = yes
}

natural_disaster_release_chain_mission_slot = {
	clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_chain_mission_active
	clear_variable = natural_disaster_chain_mission_state
	natural_disaster_schedule_capacity_refill = yes
}

natural_disaster_pay_chain_prevention = {
	controller = {
		if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.tsunami } } }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_manpower_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.rescue_manpower }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_manpower_cost = -1 }
			add_manpower = nd_chain_manpower_cost
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_truck_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.rescue_trucks }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_truck_cost = -1 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = nd_chain_truck_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_convoy_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.warning_convoys }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_convoy_cost = -1 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = convoy_1 amount = nd_chain_convoy_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_fuel_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.rescue_fuel }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_fuel_cost = -1 }
			add_fuel = nd_chain_fuel_cost
			add_stability = constant:natural_disaster_cost_fixed.rescue_stability
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.disease } } }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_manpower_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.rescue_manpower }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_manpower_cost = -1 }
			add_manpower = nd_chain_manpower_cost
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_support_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.rescue_support_equipment }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_support_cost = -1 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = nd_chain_support_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_truck_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.rescue_trucks }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_truck_cost = -1 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = nd_chain_truck_cost }
			add_stability = constant:natural_disaster_cost_fixed.rescue_stability
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.famine } } }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_manpower_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.warning_manpower }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_manpower_cost = -1 }
			add_manpower = nd_chain_manpower_cost
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_support_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.stabilize_support_equipment }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_support_cost = -1 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = nd_chain_support_cost }
			if = {
				limit = { PREV = { NOT = { natural_disaster_state_uses_convoy_recovery_route = yes } } }
				set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_transport_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.stabilize_trains }
				multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_transport_cost = -1 }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = train_equipment amount = nd_chain_transport_cost }
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_transport_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.stabilize_convoys }
				multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_transport_cost = -1 }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = convoy_1 amount = nd_chain_transport_cost }
			}
			add_stability = constant:natural_disaster_cost_fixed.stabilize_stability
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.wildfire_spread } } }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_manpower_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.rescue_manpower }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_manpower_cost = -1 }
			add_manpower = nd_chain_manpower_cost
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_support_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.rescue_support_equipment }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_support_cost = -1 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = nd_chain_support_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_truck_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.rescue_trucks }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_truck_cost = -1 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = nd_chain_truck_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_fuel_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.rescue_fuel }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_fuel_cost = -1 }
			add_fuel = nd_chain_fuel_cost
			add_stability = constant:natural_disaster_cost_fixed.rescue_stability
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.supply_collapse } } }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_support_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.stabilize_support_equipment }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_support_cost = -1 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = nd_chain_support_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_truck_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.stabilize_trucks }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_truck_cost = -1 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = nd_chain_truck_cost }
			if = {
				limit = { PREV = { NOT = { natural_disaster_state_uses_convoy_recovery_route = yes } } }
				set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_transport_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.stabilize_trains }
				multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_transport_cost = -1 }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = train_equipment amount = nd_chain_transport_cost }
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_transport_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.stabilize_convoys }
				multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_transport_cost = -1 }
				add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = convoy_1 amount = nd_chain_transport_cost }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_fuel_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.stabilize_fuel }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_fuel_cost = -1 }
			add_fuel = nd_chain_fuel_cost
			add_stability = constant:natural_disaster_cost_fixed.stabilize_stability
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { PREV = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.lahar } } }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_manpower_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.rescue_manpower }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_manpower_cost = -1 }
			add_manpower = nd_chain_manpower_cost
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_truck_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.rescue_trucks }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_truck_cost = -1 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = nd_chain_truck_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_train_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.warning_trains }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_train_cost = -1 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = train_equipment amount = nd_chain_train_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_fuel_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.rescue_fuel }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_fuel_cost = -1 }
			add_fuel = nd_chain_fuel_cost
			add_stability = constant:natural_disaster_cost_fixed.rescue_stability
		}
		else = {
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_support_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.stabilize_support_equipment }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_support_cost = -1 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = support_equipment_1 amount = nd_chain_support_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_truck_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.stabilize_trucks }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_truck_cost = -1 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = motorized_equipment_1 amount = nd_chain_truck_cost }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_chain_train_cost = constant:natural_disaster_cost.stabilize_trains }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_chain_train_cost = -1 }
			add_equipment_to_stockpile = { type = train_equipment amount = nd_chain_train_cost }
			add_stability = constant:natural_disaster_cost_fixed.stabilize_stability
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_apply_chain_prevention_work = {
	natural_disaster_achievement_record_chain_prevention = yes
	set_state_flag = natural_disaster_stabilize_chain_prevention_taken
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_prevention_score = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_prevention_score }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.early_rescue } }
		add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_score = constant:natural_disaster_warning.preparation_score }
	}
	else = {
		add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_score = constant:natural_disaster_warning.preparation_score }
	}
}

# State scope. Removes only the country mission slots targeting this card.
natural_disaster_release_state_mission_slots = {
	controller = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_active_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_1
				check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_rescue_state_1 value = PREV compare = equals }
			}
			remove_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_1
			clear_variable = natural_disaster_rescue_state_1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_active_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_2
				check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_rescue_state_2 value = PREV compare = equals }
			}
			remove_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_2
			clear_variable = natural_disaster_rescue_state_2
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_active_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_3
				check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_rescue_state_3 value = PREV compare = equals }
			}
			remove_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_3
			clear_variable = natural_disaster_rescue_state_3
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_active_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_4
				check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_rescue_state_4 value = PREV compare = equals }
			}
			remove_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_4
			clear_variable = natural_disaster_rescue_state_4
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_active_mission = natural_disaster_stabilization_mission_1
				check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_stabilization_state_1 value = PREV compare = equals }
			}
			remove_mission = natural_disaster_stabilization_mission_1
			clear_variable = natural_disaster_stabilization_state_1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_active_mission = natural_disaster_stabilization_mission_2
				check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_stabilization_state_2 value = PREV compare = equals }
			}
			remove_mission = natural_disaster_stabilization_mission_2
			clear_variable = natural_disaster_stabilization_state_2
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_active_mission = natural_disaster_stabilization_mission_3
				check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_stabilization_state_3 value = PREV compare = equals }
			}
			remove_mission = natural_disaster_stabilization_mission_3
			clear_variable = natural_disaster_stabilization_state_3
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_active_mission = natural_disaster_reconstruction_mission_1
				check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_reconstruction_state_1 value = PREV compare = equals }
			}
			remove_mission = natural_disaster_reconstruction_mission_1
			clear_variable = natural_disaster_reconstruction_state_1
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_active_mission = natural_disaster_reconstruction_mission_2
				check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_reconstruction_state_2 value = PREV compare = equals }
			}
			remove_mission = natural_disaster_reconstruction_mission_2
			clear_variable = natural_disaster_reconstruction_state_2
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_active_mission = natural_disaster_reconstruction_mission_3
				check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_reconstruction_state_3 value = PREV compare = equals }
			}
			remove_mission = natural_disaster_reconstruction_mission_3
			clear_variable = natural_disaster_reconstruction_state_3
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_release_special_state_missions = {
	controller = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = natural_disaster_chain_mission_state
				check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_chain_mission_state value = PREV compare = equals }
			}
			if = { limit = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_tsunami_mission } remove_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_tsunami_mission }
			if = { limit = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_disease_mission } remove_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_disease_mission }
			if = { limit = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_famine_mission } remove_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_famine_mission }
			if = { limit = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_wildfire_spread_mission } remove_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_wildfire_spread_mission }
			if = { limit = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_supply_collapse_mission } remove_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_supply_collapse_mission }
			if = { limit = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_lahar_mission } remove_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_lahar_mission }
			if = { limit = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_aftershock_mission } remove_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_aftershock_mission }
			if = { limit = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_chain_mission } remove_mission = natural_disaster_chain_mission }
			natural_disaster_release_chain_mission_slot = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_state
				check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_state value = PREV compare = equals }
			}
			if = { limit = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_mission } remove_mission = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_mission }
			clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_active
			clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_route_secured
			clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_route_convoy
			clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_route_rail
			clear_variable = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_state
			clear_variable = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_donor
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_schedule_reassessment_for_state = {
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_next_reassessment = global.date }
	add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_next_reassessment = natural_disaster_reassessment_delay }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_type = constant:natural_disaster_job_type.reassessment }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_delay_days = natural_disaster_reassessment_delay }
	natural_disaster_enqueue_state_job = yes
}

# State scope. Commits the already selected presentation state.
natural_disaster_commit_category_display_from_state = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_category_family = natural_disaster_family }
	controller = {
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_category_display_family = natural_disaster_category_family }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_category_display_state = PREV }
	}
}

# Country scope. Selects one live warning/aftermath with the shared recovery
# priority score. Abnormal cards outrank ordinary cards and active warnings
# outrank recovery work at the same abnormality level.
natural_disaster_refresh_category_display_family = {
	clear_variable = natural_disaster_category_display_family
	clear_variable = natural_disaster_category_display_state
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_category_display_selected = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_category_display_best_score = -1 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_variant = 0 }
	every_controlled_state = {
		limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_displayable_activity = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_score = 0 }
		natural_disaster_score_priority_state = yes
		if = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_active }
			add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_score = constant:natural_disaster_priority.presentation_warning_bonus }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_family > constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact } }
			add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_score = constant:natural_disaster_priority.presentation_abnormal_bonus }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_priority_score > natural_disaster_category_display_best_score } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_category_display_best_score = natural_disaster_priority_score }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_category_display_selected = 1 }
			save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_category_display_state_candidate
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_category_display_selected > 0 }
			has_event_target = natural_disaster_category_display_state_candidate
		}
		event_target:natural_disaster_category_display_state_candidate = {
			natural_disaster_commit_category_display_from_state = yes
		}
	}
}

# State-scope refresh hook used by warning and impact transitions.
natural_disaster_set_category_display_from_state = {
	controller = { natural_disaster_refresh_category_display_family = yes }
}

natural_disaster_refresh_country_notification_state = {
	natural_disaster_refresh_category_display_family = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { has_country_flag = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_active }
			NOT = {
				any_controlled_state = {
					OR = {
						has_state_flag = natural_disaster_impact_scheduled
						has_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_scheduled
						has_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_active
						natural_disaster_state_has_open_card = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_category_visible
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_warning_unread
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_unread
	}
}

natural_disaster_clear_pending_resilience = {
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_resilient_rails_pending
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_seismic_retrofit_pending
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_coastal_barriers_pending
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_firebreak_network_pending
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_volcanic_exclusion_routes_pending
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_water_security_pending
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_exclusion_cordon_pending
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_weather_shelter_network_pending
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_slope_stabilization_pending
}

natural_disaster_commit_full_resilience = {
	if = { limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_resilient_rails_pending } set_state_flag = natural_disaster_resilient_rails clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_resilient_rails_partial }
	if = { limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_seismic_retrofit_pending } set_state_flag = natural_disaster_seismic_retrofit clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_seismic_retrofit_partial }
	if = { limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_coastal_barriers_pending } set_state_flag = natural_disaster_coastal_barriers clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_coastal_barriers_partial }
	if = { limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_firebreak_network_pending } set_state_flag = natural_disaster_firebreak_network clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_firebreak_network_partial }
	if = { limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_volcanic_exclusion_routes_pending } set_state_flag = natural_disaster_volcanic_exclusion_routes clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_volcanic_exclusion_routes_partial }
	if = { limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_water_security_pending } set_state_flag = natural_disaster_water_security clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_water_security_partial }
	if = { limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_exclusion_cordon_pending } set_state_flag = natural_disaster_exclusion_cordon clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_exclusion_cordon_partial }
	if = { limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_weather_shelter_network_pending } set_state_flag = natural_disaster_weather_shelter_network clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_weather_shelter_network_partial }
	if = { limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_slope_stabilization_pending } set_state_flag = natural_disaster_slope_stabilization clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_slope_stabilization_partial }
	natural_disaster_clear_pending_resilience = yes
}

natural_disaster_commit_partial_resilience = {
	if = { limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_resilient_rails_pending } set_state_flag = natural_disaster_resilient_rails_partial }
	if = { limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_seismic_retrofit_pending } set_state_flag = natural_disaster_seismic_retrofit_partial }
	if = { limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_coastal_barriers_pending } set_state_flag = natural_disaster_coastal_barriers_partial }
	if = { limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_firebreak_network_pending } set_state_flag = natural_disaster_firebreak_network_partial }
	if = { limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_volcanic_exclusion_routes_pending } set_state_flag = natural_disaster_volcanic_exclusion_routes_partial }
	if = { limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_water_security_pending } set_state_flag = natural_disaster_water_security_partial }
	if = { limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_exclusion_cordon_pending } set_state_flag = natural_disaster_exclusion_cordon_partial }
	if = { limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_weather_shelter_network_pending } set_state_flag = natural_disaster_weather_shelter_network_partial }
	if = { limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_slope_stabilization_pending } set_state_flag = natural_disaster_slope_stabilization_partial }
	natural_disaster_clear_pending_resilience = yes
}

natural_disaster_close_aftermath_card = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_closing_internal_card = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_only_card
				has_state_flag = natural_disaster_ocean_impact_proxy
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_closing_internal_card = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_closing_internal_card < 1 } }
		natural_disaster_achievement_track_card_close = yes
	}
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_close_with_partial_strain = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_closed_abnormal_card = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_reconstruction_partial }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_close_with_partial_strain = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_card }
		clr_state_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_card
		clr_state_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_action_1
		clr_state_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_action_2
		clr_state_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_action_3
		clr_state_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_action_4
		clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_refugee_disease_pending
		clear_variable = achievement_nd_refugee_counted_sequence
		clear_variable = achievement_nd_refugee_recovered_sequence
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_abnormal_gui_card }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_closed_abnormal_card = 1 }
		clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_abnormal_gui_card
		controller = {
			remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_abnormal_states value = PREV }
		}
	}
	natural_disaster_release_state_mission_slots = yes
	natural_disaster_release_special_state_missions = yes
	natural_disaster_clear_family_disruption_modifiers = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_close_with_partial_strain > 0 } }
		multiply_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_supply = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.partial_recovery_strain }
		multiply_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_speed = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.partial_recovery_strain }
		multiply_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_repair = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.partial_recovery_strain }
		multiply_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_resources = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.partial_recovery_strain }
		set_state_flag = {
			flag = natural_disaster_partial_recovery_strain
			days = @ND_PARTIAL_RECOVERY_STRAIN_DAYS
		}
		add_dynamic_modifier = { modifier = natural_disaster_partial_recovery_strain }
	}
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_active
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_neighbor_aftermath
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_heat_aftermath
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_active
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_stabilize_chain_prevention_taken
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_partial_prevention
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_mission_success
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_mission_failed
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_terminal_failure_consequence
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_recovery_responsibility_transferred
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_only_card
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_ocean_impact_proxy
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_rescue_search_completed
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_shelters_opened
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_rescue_route_cleared
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_emergency_evacuation_completed
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_medical_triage_completed
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_port_lifeline_established
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_clean_water_secured
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_relief_rail_restored
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_relief_port_reopened
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_food_corridor_secured
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_factory_inspection_complete
	natural_disaster_clear_pending_resilience = yes
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.closed }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.closed }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_path_status = constant:natural_disaster_path_status.closed }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_cleanup_state = 1 }
	natural_disaster_update_abnormal_history_record = yes
	remove_from_array = { array = global.natural_disaster_open_card_states value = THIS }
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_recovery_unresolved_territory
	controller = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_closed_abnormal_card > 0 }
				has_country_flag = natural_disaster_abnormal_map_open
			}
			natural_disaster_rebuild_abnormal_gui_view = yes
		}
		remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_active_states value = PREV }
		natural_disaster_unregister_relief_recipient_country_if_inactive = yes
		natural_disaster_refresh_aftermath_capacity = yes
		natural_disaster_fill_open_aftermath_capacity = yes
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { natural_disaster_country_has_open_aftermath = yes } }
			remove_ideas = natural_disaster_aftermath_idea
			clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_unread
		}
		natural_disaster_refresh_country_notification_state = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_closing_internal_card < 1 } }
			natural_disaster_achievement_finalize_country_recovery = yes
		}
	}
}

# Country scope. Migrates every pending queue row for the transferred state to
# the new responsible country without changing its globally reserved due date.
natural_disaster_transfer_pending_jobs_for_state = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_index = 0 }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = natural_disaster_transfer_job_index
				value = natural_disaster_job_target_state_entries^num
				compare = less_than
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = {
					var = natural_disaster_job_target_state_entries^natural_disaster_transfer_job_index
					value = event_target:natural_disaster_transfer_state
					compare = equals
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_type = natural_disaster_job_type_entries^natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_sequence_id = natural_disaster_job_sequence_id_entries^natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_due_date = natural_disaster_job_due_date_entries^natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_family = natural_disaster_job_family_entries^natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_followup_route = natural_disaster_job_followup_route_entries^natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_severity = natural_disaster_job_severity_entries^natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_deaths = natural_disaster_job_deaths_entries^natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_damage_transport = natural_disaster_job_damage_transport_entries^natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_damage_industry = natural_disaster_job_damage_industry_entries^natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_damage_ports = natural_disaster_job_damage_ports_entries^natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_damage_air = natural_disaster_job_damage_air_entries^natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_damage_resources = natural_disaster_job_damage_resources_entries^natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_damage_shelter = natural_disaster_job_damage_shelter_entries^natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_warning_choice = natural_disaster_job_warning_choice_entries^natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_warning_outcome = natural_disaster_job_warning_outcome_entries^natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_warning_matched = natural_disaster_job_warning_matched_entries^natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_phase = natural_disaster_job_phase_entries^natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_segment = natural_disaster_job_segment_entries^natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_chain_due_date = natural_disaster_job_chain_due_date_entries^natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_chain_target_state = natural_disaster_job_chain_target_state_entries^natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_has_chain_target = natural_disaster_job_has_chain_target_entries^natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_origin_family = natural_disaster_job_origin_family_entries^natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_origin_medium = natural_disaster_job_origin_medium_entries^natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_skyfall_variant = natural_disaster_job_skyfall_variant_entries^natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_path_basin = natural_disaster_job_path_basin_entries^natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_target_state_entries index = natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_type_entries index = natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_sequence_id_entries index = natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_due_date_entries index = natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_family_entries index = natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_followup_route_entries index = natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_severity_entries index = natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_deaths_entries index = natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_damage_transport_entries index = natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_damage_industry_entries index = natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_damage_ports_entries index = natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_damage_air_entries index = natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_damage_resources_entries index = natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_damage_shelter_entries index = natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_warning_choice_entries index = natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_warning_outcome_entries index = natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_warning_matched_entries index = natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_phase_entries index = natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_segment_entries index = natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_chain_due_date_entries index = natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_chain_target_state_entries index = natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_has_chain_target_entries index = natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_origin_family_entries index = natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_origin_medium_entries index = natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_skyfall_variant_entries index = natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_path_basin_entries index = natural_disaster_transfer_job_index }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_delay = natural_disaster_transfer_job_due_date }
			subtract_from_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_delay = global.date }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_delay < 0 } }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_delay = 0 }
			}
			event_target:natural_disaster_new_responsible_country = {
				add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_target_state_entries value = event_target:natural_disaster_transfer_state }
				add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_type_entries value = natural_disaster_transfer_job_type }
				add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_sequence_id_entries value = natural_disaster_transfer_job_sequence_id }
				add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_due_date_entries value = natural_disaster_transfer_job_due_date }
				add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_family_entries value = natural_disaster_transfer_job_family }
				add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_followup_route_entries value = natural_disaster_transfer_job_followup_route }
				add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_severity_entries value = natural_disaster_transfer_job_severity }
				add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_deaths_entries value = natural_disaster_transfer_job_deaths }
				add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_damage_transport_entries value = natural_disaster_transfer_job_damage_transport }
				add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_damage_industry_entries value = natural_disaster_transfer_job_damage_industry }
				add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_damage_ports_entries value = natural_disaster_transfer_job_damage_ports }
				add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_damage_air_entries value = natural_disaster_transfer_job_damage_air }
				add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_damage_resources_entries value = natural_disaster_transfer_job_damage_resources }
				add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_damage_shelter_entries value = natural_disaster_transfer_job_damage_shelter }
				add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_warning_choice_entries value = natural_disaster_transfer_job_warning_choice }
				add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_warning_outcome_entries value = natural_disaster_transfer_job_warning_outcome }
				add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_warning_matched_entries value = natural_disaster_transfer_job_warning_matched }
				add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_phase_entries value = natural_disaster_transfer_job_phase }
				add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_segment_entries value = natural_disaster_transfer_job_segment }
				add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_chain_due_date_entries value = natural_disaster_transfer_job_chain_due_date }
				add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_chain_target_state_entries value = natural_disaster_transfer_job_chain_target_state }
				add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_has_chain_target_entries value = natural_disaster_transfer_job_has_chain_target }
				add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_origin_family_entries value = natural_disaster_transfer_job_origin_family }
				add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_origin_medium_entries value = natural_disaster_transfer_job_origin_medium }
				add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_skyfall_variant_entries value = natural_disaster_transfer_job_skyfall_variant }
				add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_job_path_basin_entries value = natural_disaster_transfer_job_path_basin }
				set_country_flag = natural_disaster_queue_active
				country_event = {
					id = chaosx.nr13.2
					days = natural_disaster_transfer_job_delay
				}
			}
		}
		else = {
			add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_job_index = 1 }
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_job_due_date_entries^num < 1 } }
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_queue_active
	}
}

# Country scope. Removes every mission, pointer, and visible-card reference
# held by this country for the state saved as natural_disaster_transfer_state.
natural_disaster_release_transferred_state_pointers = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_slot = 1 }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = natural_disaster_transfer_slot
				value = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.maximum_rescue_cap
				compare = less_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		meta_effect = {
			text = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_active_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_[SLOT]
						has_variable = natural_disaster_rescue_state_[SLOT]
						check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_rescue_state_[SLOT] value = event_target:natural_disaster_transfer_state compare = equals }
					}
					remove_mission = natural_disaster_rescue_mission_[SLOT]
					clear_variable = natural_disaster_rescue_state_[SLOT]
				}
			}
			SLOT = "[?natural_disaster_transfer_slot|.0]"
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_slot = 1 }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_slot = 1 }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = natural_disaster_transfer_slot
				value = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.maximum_stabilization_cap
				compare = less_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		meta_effect = {
			text = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_active_mission = natural_disaster_stabilization_mission_[SLOT]
						has_variable = natural_disaster_stabilization_state_[SLOT]
						check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_stabilization_state_[SLOT] value = event_target:natural_disaster_transfer_state compare = equals }
					}
					remove_mission = natural_disaster_stabilization_mission_[SLOT]
					clear_variable = natural_disaster_stabilization_state_[SLOT]
				}
			}
			SLOT = "[?natural_disaster_transfer_slot|.0]"
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_slot = 1 }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_slot = 1 }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = {
				var = natural_disaster_transfer_slot
				value = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.maximum_reconstruction_cap
				compare = less_than_or_equals
			}
		}
		meta_effect = {
			text = {
				if = {
					limit = {
						has_active_mission = natural_disaster_reconstruction_mission_[SLOT]
						has_variable = natural_disaster_reconstruction_state_[SLOT]
						check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_reconstruction_state_[SLOT] value = event_target:natural_disaster_transfer_state compare = equals }
					}
					remove_mission = natural_disaster_reconstruction_mission_[SLOT]
					clear_variable = natural_disaster_reconstruction_state_[SLOT]
				}
			}
			SLOT = "[?natural_disaster_transfer_slot|.0]"
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_transfer_slot = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = natural_disaster_chain_mission_state
			check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_chain_mission_state value = event_target:natural_disaster_transfer_state compare = equals }
		}
		if = { limit = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_tsunami_mission } remove_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_tsunami_mission }
		if = { limit = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_disease_mission } remove_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_disease_mission }
		if = { limit = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_famine_mission } remove_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_famine_mission }
		if = { limit = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_wildfire_spread_mission } remove_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_wildfire_spread_mission }
		if = { limit = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_supply_collapse_mission } remove_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_supply_collapse_mission }
		if = { limit = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_lahar_mission } remove_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_lahar_mission }
		if = { limit = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_aftershock_mission } remove_mission = natural_disaster_chain_prevent_aftershock_mission }
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_chain_mission_active
		clear_variable = natural_disaster_chain_mission_state
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_state
			check_variable = { var = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_state value = event_target:natural_disaster_transfer_state compare = equals }
		}
		if = { limit = { has_active_mission = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_mission } remove_mission = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_mission }
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_active
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_route_secured
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_route_convoy
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_route_rail
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_relief_variant_neighbor_convoy
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_relief_variant_port_lifeline
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_relief_variant_engineer
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_relief_variant_medical
		clear_variable = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_state
		clear_variable = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_donor
	}
	remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_active_states value = event_target:natural_disaster_transfer_state }
	natural_disaster_unregister_relief_recipient_country_if_inactive = yes
	remove_from_array = { array = natural_disaster_abnormal_states value = event_target:natural_disaster_transfer_state }
	natural_disaster_refresh_category_display_family = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_active_states^num < 1 }
			NOT = {
				any_controlled_state = {
					NOT = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_recovery_unresolved_territory }
					OR = {
						has_state_flag = natural_disaster_impact_scheduled
						has_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_scheduled
						has_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_active
						natural_disaster_state_has_open_card = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
		remove_ideas = natural_disaster_aftermath_idea
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_category_visible
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_warning_unread
		clr_country_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_unread
	}
}

# Country scope. Registers the transferred state with its new responsible
# controller and restores only the phase and chain mission that remain live.
natural_disaster_register_transferred_state_responsibility = {
	event_target:natural_disaster_transfer_state = {
		clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_recovery_unresolved_territory
		set_state_flag = natural_disaster_recovery_responsibility_transferred
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_state_flag = natural_disaster_impact_scheduled
					has_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_scheduled
					has_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_active
				}
			}
			event_target:natural_disaster_new_responsible_country = {
				set_country_flag = natural_disaster_warning_unread
				set_country_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_category_visible
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_open_card = yes }
			event_target:natural_disaster_new_responsible_country = {
				set_country_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_unread
				set_country_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_category_visible
				if = { limit = { NOT = { has_idea = natural_disaster_aftermath_idea } } add_ideas = natural_disaster_aftermath_idea }
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = natural_disaster_active_states value = PREV } } }
					add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_active_states value = PREV }
				}
				natural_disaster_register_relief_recipient_country = yes
				natural_disaster_fill_open_aftermath_capacity = yes
				if = {
					limit = {
						PREV = {
							check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk > constant:natural_disaster_chain.none }
							check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_due_date > global.date }
						}
					}
					natural_disaster_activate_family_chain_mission = yes
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_abnormal_gui_card }
			event_target:natural_disaster_new_responsible_country = {
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = natural_disaster_abnormal_states value = PREV } } }
					add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_abnormal_states value = PREV }
				}
				set_country_flag = natural_disaster_abnormal_map_available
				set_country_flag = natural_disaster_abnormal_map_unread
			}
		}
	}
	natural_disaster_refresh_category_display_family = yes
}

natural_disaster_handle_state_control_change = {
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_event_target = natural_disaster_new_responsible_country
			OR = {
				is_owned_by = event_target:natural_disaster_new_responsible_country
				is_controlled_by = event_target:natural_disaster_new_responsible_country
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_event_target = natural_disaster_former_responsible_country }
			event_target:natural_disaster_former_responsible_country = {
				natural_disaster_transfer_pending_jobs_for_state = yes
				natural_disaster_release_transferred_state_pointers = yes
			}
		}
		clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_active
		event_target:natural_disaster_new_responsible_country = { natural_disaster_register_transferred_state_responsibility = yes }
	}
	else = {
		clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_recovery_responsibility_transferred
		set_state_flag = natural_disaster_recovery_unresolved_territory
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.degraded }
		if = {
			limit = { has_event_target = natural_disaster_former_responsible_country }
			event_target:natural_disaster_former_responsible_country = { natural_disaster_release_transferred_state_pointers = yes }
		}
		clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_inbound_relief_active
		controller = {
			set_country_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_category_visible
			set_country_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_unread
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_clear_heat_overlap_for_country = {
	every_controlled_state = {
		limit = {
			has_state_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_active
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.heat_wave }
		}
		natural_disaster_close_aftermath_card = yes
	}
}

# State scope. Follow-up policy is independent from aftermath policy. When a
# caller requests no recovery card but does request a live chain, this bounded
# warning card exposes the exact route and mission until the chain resolves; it
# applies no recovery disruption, reassessment, or country aftermath idea.
natural_disaster_activate_chain_only_card = {
	set_state_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_active
	set_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_only_card
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = global.natural_disaster_open_card_states value = THIS } } }
		add_to_array = { array = global.natural_disaster_open_card_states value = THIS }
	}
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.active }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.warning }
	natural_disaster_set_category_display_from_state = yes
	controller = {
		set_country_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_category_visible
		set_country_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_unread
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = natural_disaster_active_states value = PREV } } }
			add_to_array = { array = natural_disaster_active_states value = PREV }
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_activate_aftermath_for_state = {
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_aftermath_policy = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath_policy.none } } }
		set_state_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_active
		air_contamination_register_natural_disaster_aftermath_source = yes
		if = {
			limit = { NOT = { is_in_array = { array = global.natural_disaster_open_card_states value = THIS } } }
			add_to_array = { array = global.natural_disaster_open_card_states value = THIS }
		}
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.active }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.early_rescue }
		natural_disaster_set_category_display_from_state = yes
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.none } }
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_path_status = constant:natural_disaster_path_status.recovery }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_reassessment_delay = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.rescue_days }
		natural_disaster_schedule_reassessment_for_state = yes
		natural_disaster_apply_state_disruption = yes
		controller = { save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_current_state_controller }
		if = {
			limit = { is_controlled_by = event_target:natural_disaster_current_state_controller }
			clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_recovery_unresolved_territory
			controller = {
				set_country_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_unread
				if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_idea = natural_disaster_aftermath_idea } }
					add_ideas = natural_disaster_aftermath_idea
				}
				if = {
					limit = {
						NOT = {
							is_in_array = {
								array = natural_disaster_active_states
								value = PREV
							}
						}
					}
					add_to_array = {
						array = natural_disaster_active_states
						value = PREV
					}
				}
				natural_disaster_register_relief_recipient_country = yes
				natural_disaster_fill_open_aftermath_capacity = yes
			}
		}
		else = {
			set_state_flag = natural_disaster_recovery_unresolved_territory
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.degraded }
			controller = {
				set_country_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_category_visible
				set_country_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_unread
			}
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_schedule_family_report = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_ceiling = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.report_max_days }
	add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_ceiling = 1 }
	set_temp_variable_to_random = {
		var = natural_disaster_report_delay
		min = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.report_min_days
		max = natural_disaster_random_ceiling
		integer = yes
	}
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_type = constant:natural_disaster_job_type.report }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_delay_days = natural_disaster_report_delay }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_due_date = 0 }
	natural_disaster_enqueue_state_job = yes
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_report_due_date = natural_disaster_job_due_date }
	natural_disaster_update_abnormal_history_record = yes
}

natural_disaster_schedule_family_news = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_should_news = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = {
			NOT = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_news_policy = constant:natural_disaster_news_policy.none } }
			NOT = {
				meta_trigger = {
					text = { has_global_flag = natural_disaster_news_cooldown_[FAMILY_ID] }
					FAMILY_ID = "[?natural_disaster_current_family|.0]"
				}
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_news_policy = constant:natural_disaster_news_policy.always } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_should_news = 1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_news_policy = constant:natural_disaster_news_policy.major_only }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity > constant:natural_disaster_severity.regional }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_should_news = 1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
