				check_variable = { natural_disaster_news_policy = constant:natural_disaster_news_policy.first_family }
				NOT = {
					meta_trigger = {
						text = { has_global_flag = natural_disaster_family_seen_[FAMILY_ID] }
						FAMILY_ID = "[?natural_disaster_current_family|.0]"
					}
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_should_news = 1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_news_policy = constant:natural_disaster_news_policy.meaningful }
				OR = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity > constant:natural_disaster_severity.local }
					OWNER = { is_ai = no }
					is_capital = yes
					NOT = {
						meta_trigger = {
							text = { has_global_flag = natural_disaster_family_seen_[FAMILY_ID] }
							FAMILY_ID = "[?natural_disaster_current_family|.0]"
						}
					}
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_should_news = 1 }
		}
	}

	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_should_news > 0 } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_type = constant:natural_disaster_job_type.news }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_delay_days = constant:natural_disaster_threshold.minimum_job_delay_days }
		natural_disaster_enqueue_state_job = yes
		meta_effect = {
			text = {
				set_global_flag = {
					flag = natural_disaster_news_cooldown_[FAMILY_ID]
					days = [COOLDOWN_DAYS]
				}
			}
			FAMILY_ID = "[?natural_disaster_current_family|.0]"
			COOLDOWN_DAYS = "[?natural_disaster_news_cooldown_days|.0]"
		}
	}
	meta_effect = {
		text = { set_global_flag = natural_disaster_family_seen_[FAMILY_ID] }
		FAMILY_ID = "[?natural_disaster_current_family|.0]"
	}
}

# State scope. Physical follow-ups retain their chain mission on the source
# card, but their damaging subevent is assigned to a geography-valid origin or
# adjacent state. Socioeconomic chains keep the source state.
natural_disaster_prepare_chain_target = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_can_schedule = 1 }
	clear_variable = natural_disaster_chain_target_state
	clear_variable = natural_disaster_chain_family
	clear_variable = natural_disaster_chain_source_severity
	if = {
		limit = { natural_disaster_chain_is_physical = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_can_schedule = 0 }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_source_family = natural_disaster_current_family }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_active_origin_medium = constant:natural_disaster_origin_medium.none }
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_variable = natural_disaster_origin_state
				has_variable = natural_disaster_origin_family
			}
			var:natural_disaster_origin_state = { save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_active_origin_state }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_active_origin_family = natural_disaster_origin_family }
		}
		else = {
			save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_active_origin_state
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_active_origin_family = natural_disaster_chain_source_family }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_origin_medium }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_active_origin_medium = natural_disaster_origin_medium }
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_active_origin_saved = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.aftershock }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.aftershock_damage }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.global_aftershocks }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.seismic_landslide }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.regional_landslides }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dry_mass_movement }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.offshore_tsunami }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.tsunami }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.delayed_tsunami_chain }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.urban_fire }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.wildfire_spread }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.wildfire_line }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.wildfire_ignition }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.wildfire }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wildfire }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.dam_failure_flash_flood }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.inland_flood }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.flash_flood }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.flood_renewal }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.flood }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.flood }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.storm_surge } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.storm_surge }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.severe_thunderstorm_tail }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.thunderstorm_return }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.thunderstorm }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.hailstorm }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.skyfire_hail }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.hailstorm }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.extreme_cold_wave } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.cold_wave }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.blizzard_followup } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.blizzard }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.drought }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.drought_feedback }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.drought }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.heat_wave } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.heat_wave }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.ashfall }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.regional_ashfall }
				}
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.ashfall }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.lahar } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.lahar }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.dust_veil } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.dust_and_sandstorm }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.meteor_shower } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_shower }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.meteor_impact } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.meteor_impact }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.tornado_outbreak } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tornado_outbreak }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.aftershock } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.tsunami } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.lahar } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.lahar }
		}
		else = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wildfire } }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_family = natural_disaster_current_family }
		if = {
			limit = {
				natural_disaster_is_family_geographically_eligible = yes
				natural_disaster_family_origin_context_is_valid = yes
				natural_disaster_family_initiating_context_is_valid = yes
			}
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_target_state = THIS }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_can_schedule = 1 }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				any_neighbor_state = { natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes }
			}
			random_neighbor_state = {
				limit = { natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_target_scope = THIS }
				PREV = { set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_target_state = natural_disaster_chain_target_scope } }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_can_schedule = 1 }
			}
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = natural_disaster_chain_source_family }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_can_schedule < 1 } }
			set_state_flag = natural_disaster_physical_chain_skipped
			add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_skipped_physical_chains = 1 }
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.none }
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.none }
			clear_variable = natural_disaster_chain_target_state
		}
	}
}

natural_disaster_schedule_chain_followup = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_can_schedule = 0 }
	natural_disaster_prepare_chain_target = yes
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_can_schedule > 0 }
			natural_disaster_state_has_open_card = yes
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk > constant:natural_disaster_chain.none }
		}
		natural_disaster_achievement_track_chain_schedule = yes
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_source_severity = natural_disaster_current_severity }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_minimum = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_political_min_days }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_ceiling = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_political_days }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.tsunami } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_minimum = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_tsunami_min_days }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_ceiling = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_tsunami_days }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.disease } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_minimum = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_disease_min_days }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_ceiling = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_disease_days }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.famine } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_minimum = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_famine_min_days }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_ceiling = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_famine_days }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.wildfire_spread } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_minimum = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_wildfire_min_days }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_ceiling = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_wildfire_days }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.supply_collapse } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_minimum = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_supply_min_days }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_ceiling = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_supply_days }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.lahar } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_minimum = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_lahar_min_days }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_ceiling = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_lahar_days }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.aftershock } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_minimum = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_aftershock_min_days }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_ceiling = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_aftershock_days }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.refugee_pressure } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_minimum = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_refugee_min_days }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_ceiling = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_refugee_days }
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_ceiling = 1 }
		set_temp_variable_to_random = {
			var = natural_disaster_chain_delay
			min = natural_disaster_random_minimum
			max = natural_disaster_random_ceiling
			integer = yes
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_type = constant:natural_disaster_job_type.followup }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_delay_days = natural_disaster_chain_delay }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_due_date = 0 }
		natural_disaster_enqueue_state_job = yes
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_due_date = natural_disaster_job_due_date }
		natural_disaster_update_abnormal_history_record = yes
		controller = {
			natural_disaster_activate_family_chain_mission = yes
		}
	}
}

# State scope. Converts a timed objective's full, partial, or failed result
# into separate human, material, and disruption multipliers before the queued
# chain is resolved. Full prevention is handled by clearing the chain itself.
natural_disaster_prepare_chain_outcome_scaling = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_death_outcome = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_building_outcome = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_disruption_outcome = 1 }
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_partial_prevention }
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.tsunami } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_death_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.tsunami_partial_death }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_building_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.tsunami_partial_building }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_disruption_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.tsunami_partial_disruption }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.disease } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_death_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.disease_partial_death }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_building_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.disease_partial_building }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_disruption_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.disease_partial_disruption }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.famine } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_death_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.famine_partial_death }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_building_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.famine_partial_building }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_disruption_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.famine_partial_disruption }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.wildfire_spread } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_death_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.wildfire_partial_death }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_building_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.wildfire_partial_building }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_disruption_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.wildfire_partial_disruption }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.supply_collapse } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_death_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.supply_partial_death }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_building_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.supply_partial_building }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_disruption_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.supply_partial_disruption }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.lahar } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_death_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.lahar_partial_death }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_building_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.lahar_partial_building }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_disruption_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.lahar_partial_disruption }
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.aftershock } }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_death_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.aftershock_partial_death }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_building_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.aftershock_partial_building }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_disruption_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.aftershock_partial_disruption }
		}
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_mission_failed }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_death_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.failed_death }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_building_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.failed_building }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_disruption_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.failed_disruption }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_phase_failed }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_death_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.failed_recovery_scale }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_building_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.failed_recovery_scale }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_disruption_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.failed_recovery_scale }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_phase_partial }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_death_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.partial_recovery_scale }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_building_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.partial_recovery_scale }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_disruption_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.partial_recovery_scale }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { natural_disaster_warning_protection_matches_chain = yes }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_death_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.matched_warning_death }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_building_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.matched_warning_building }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_disruption_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_chain_outcome.matched_warning_disruption }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_state_flag = natural_disaster_shelters_opened
				has_state_flag = natural_disaster_emergency_evacuation_completed
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.tsunami }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.wildfire_spread }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.lahar }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.refugee_pressure }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.disease }
			}
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_death_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_action_outcome.population_death }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_building_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_action_outcome.population_building }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_disruption_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_action_outcome.population_disruption }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			OR = {
				has_state_flag = natural_disaster_rescue_route_cleared
				has_state_flag = natural_disaster_port_lifeline_established
				has_state_flag = natural_disaster_relief_rail_restored
				has_state_flag = natural_disaster_relief_port_reopened
			}
			OR = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.supply_collapse }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.tsunami }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.lahar }
			}
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_death_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_action_outcome.transport_death }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_building_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_action_outcome.transport_building }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_disruption_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_action_outcome.transport_disruption }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.disease }
			OR = {
				has_state_flag = natural_disaster_medical_triage_completed
				has_state_flag = natural_disaster_clean_water_secured
				has_state_flag = natural_disaster_foreign_medical_mission
			}
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_death_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_action_outcome.medical_death }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_building_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_action_outcome.medical_building }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_disruption_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_action_outcome.medical_disruption }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.famine }
			has_state_flag = natural_disaster_food_corridor_secured
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_death_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_action_outcome.food_death }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_building_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_action_outcome.food_building }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_disruption_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_action_outcome.food_disruption }
	}
	if = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.aftershock }
			OR = {
				has_state_flag = natural_disaster_rescue_search_completed
				has_state_flag = natural_disaster_factory_inspection_complete
			}
		}
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_death_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_action_outcome.inspection_death }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_building_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_action_outcome.inspection_building }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_disruption_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_action_outcome.inspection_disruption }
	}
}

# State scope. A matured physical chain re-enters the public API with an exact
# geography-validated target, no history ownership, and chain-scaled damage.
# This preserves the single Event 013 history row while avoiding a second copy
# of impact, report, news, aftermath, and recovery logic.
natural_disaster_dispatch_physical_chain_call = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_handled_externally = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_call_target_valid = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_result = constant:natural_disaster_call_result.rejected }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_id = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_source_family = natural_disaster_family }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_source_route = natural_disaster_followup_route }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_source_origin_medium = natural_disaster_origin_medium }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_source_skyfall_variant = natural_disaster_skyfall_variant }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_source_sequence_id = natural_disaster_sequence_id }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_source_evolution = natural_disaster_evolution }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_source_segment = natural_disaster_path_segment_index }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_source_planned_hits = natural_disaster_sequence_planned_hits }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_segment_offset = natural_disaster_chain_source_segment }
	multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_segment_offset = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.causal_segment_stride }
	add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_segment_offset = natural_disaster_chain_source_planned_hits }
	clamp_temp_variable = {
		var = natural_disaster_chain_segment_offset
		min = 0
		max = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.maximum_causal_segment_offset
	}
	save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_call_internal_chain_source_state
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = natural_disaster_origin_state
			has_variable = natural_disaster_origin_family
		}
		var:natural_disaster_origin_state = {
			save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_active_origin_state
			save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_call_origin_state
		}
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_active_origin_family = natural_disaster_origin_family }
	}
	else = {
		save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_active_origin_state
		save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_call_origin_state
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_active_origin_family = natural_disaster_chain_source_family }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_active_origin_saved = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_active_origin_medium = natural_disaster_chain_source_origin_medium }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_origin_state_supplied = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_origin_family = natural_disaster_active_origin_family }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_origin_medium = natural_disaster_active_origin_medium }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_call_family = natural_disaster_chain_family }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_chain_source_severity }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_call_severity = natural_disaster_chain_source_severity }
	}
	else = { set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_call_severity = natural_disaster_severity } }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_call_aftermath_policy = natural_disaster_aftermath_policy }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_call_recovery_scale = natural_disaster_recovery_scale }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_call_warning_scale = natural_disaster_warning_scale }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_death_outcome = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_building_outcome = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_disruption_outcome = 1 }
	natural_disaster_prepare_chain_outcome_scaling = yes
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_impact_scale = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.baseline_impact_scale }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution > 1 } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_impact_scale = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.evolution_ii_impact_scale }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution > 2 } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_impact_scale = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.evolution_iii_impact_scale }
	}
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_call_death_scale = natural_disaster_death_scale }
	multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_call_death_scale = natural_disaster_chain_impact_scale }
	multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_call_death_scale = natural_disaster_chain_death_outcome }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_call_building_scale = natural_disaster_building_scale }
	multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_call_building_scale = natural_disaster_chain_impact_scale }
	multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_call_building_scale = natural_disaster_chain_building_outcome }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_call_supply_scale = natural_disaster_supply_scale }
	multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_call_supply_scale = natural_disaster_chain_impact_scale }
	multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_call_supply_scale = natural_disaster_chain_disruption_outcome }
	if = {
		limit = {
			has_variable = natural_disaster_chain_target_state
			has_variable = natural_disaster_chain_family
		}
		var:natural_disaster_chain_target_state = {
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = natural_disaster_chain_call_family }
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes
						natural_disaster_is_valid_repeat_impact_state = yes
					}
				}
				save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_call_target_state
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_call_target_valid = 1 }
			}
		}
	}
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = natural_disaster_chain_source_family }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_call_target_valid > 0 } }
		controller = {
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_type = constant:natural_disaster_caller.external_event }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_caller_event_id = constant:natural_disaster_event.id }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family = natural_disaster_chain_call_family }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_family_group = constant:natural_disaster_family_group.random }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_mode = constant:natural_disaster_target_mode.selected_state }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_state_supplied = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_target_country_supplied = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_severity = natural_disaster_chain_call_severity }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_source_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.delayed_tsunami_chain } }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_mode = constant:natural_disaster_sequence_mode.moving_corridor }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_count = constant:natural_disaster_abnormal.large_tsunami_path_hits }
			}
			else = {
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_mode = constant:natural_disaster_sequence_mode.single }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_count = 1 }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_news_policy = constant:natural_disaster_news_policy.meaningful }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_report_policy = constant:natural_disaster_report_policy.affected_country }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_aftermath_policy = natural_disaster_chain_call_aftermath_policy }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_chain_policy = constant:natural_disaster_chain_policy.none }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_death_scale = natural_disaster_chain_call_death_scale }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_building_scale = natural_disaster_chain_call_building_scale }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_warning_scale = natural_disaster_chain_call_warning_scale }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_recovery_scale = natural_disaster_chain_call_recovery_scale }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_supply_scale = natural_disaster_chain_call_supply_scale }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_log_mode = constant:natural_disaster_log_mode.none }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_evolution_override_supplied = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_evolution_override = natural_disaster_chain_source_evolution }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_internal_chain_override = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_causal_context_supplied = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_causal_origin_medium = natural_disaster_chain_source_origin_medium }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_causal_skyfall_variant = natural_disaster_chain_source_skyfall_variant }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_id_override_supplied = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_sequence_id_override = natural_disaster_chain_source_sequence_id }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_segment_offset_supplied = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_segment_offset = natural_disaster_chain_segment_offset }
			call_natural_disaster = yes
		}
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_call_result = constant:natural_disaster_call_result.accepted } }
		set_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_arrived
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.chained }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_physical_chain_sequence_id = natural_disaster_call_sequence_id }
		natural_disaster_achievement_track_chain_arrival_before = yes
	}
	else = {
		set_state_flag = natural_disaster_physical_chain_skipped
		add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_skipped_physical_chains = 1 }
	}
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.none }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_path_status = constant:natural_disaster_path_status.recovery }
	clear_variable = natural_disaster_chain_target_state
	clear_variable = natural_disaster_chain_family
	clear_variable = natural_disaster_chain_source_severity
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_partial_prevention
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_mission_success
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_mission_failed
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_stabilize_chain_prevention_taken
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_prevention_score = 0 }
	controller = { natural_disaster_refresh_category_display_family = yes }
}

natural_disaster_execute_chain_followup = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_death_mult = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_building_mult = 1 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resilience_disruption_mult = 1 }
	event_target:natural_disaster_impact_state = {
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_handled_externally = 0 }
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_prevention_score > 0 }
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk > constant:natural_disaster_chain.none }
			}
			set_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_prevented
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.none }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_state_flag = natural_disaster_refugee_disease_pending
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.disease }
				OR = {
					has_state_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_action_1
					has_state_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_action_2
					has_state_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_action_3
					has_state_flag = achievement_nd_refugee_action_4
				}
			}
			clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_refugee_disease_pending
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.none }
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_state_flag = natural_disaster_refugee_disease_pending
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.none }
			}
			clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_refugee_disease_pending
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				has_state_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_active
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk > constant:natural_disaster_chain.none }
				natural_disaster_chain_is_physical = yes
			}
			natural_disaster_dispatch_physical_chain_call = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_handled_externally < 1 }
				has_state_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_active
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk > constant:natural_disaster_chain.none }
			}
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.chained }
			set_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_arrived
			set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_chain_type = natural_disaster_chain_risk }
			natural_disaster_achievement_track_chain_arrival_before = yes
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = natural_disaster_family }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.aftershock } }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.earthquake }
				set_state_flag = natural_disaster_aftershock_active
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.tsunami } }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.tsunami }
				set_state_flag = natural_disaster_tsunami_aftermath
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.lahar } }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.lahar }
				set_state_flag = natural_disaster_lahar_aftermath
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.wildfire_spread } }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.wildfire }
				set_state_flag = natural_disaster_wildfire_spread
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.famine } }
				set_state_flag = natural_disaster_famine_pressure
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.disease } }
				set_state_flag = natural_disaster_disease_pressure
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.refugee_pressure } }
				set_state_flag = natural_disaster_refugee_pressure
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.supply_collapse } }
				set_state_flag = natural_disaster_supply_collapse
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.political_shock } }
				set_state_flag = natural_disaster_political_shock
				OWNER = {
					add_stability = constant:natural_disaster_consequence.political_shock_stability
					add_war_support = constant:natural_disaster_consequence.political_shock_war_support
				}
			}

			set_variable = { natural_disaster_family = natural_disaster_current_family }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity = natural_disaster_severity }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = natural_disaster_evolution }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_family_lethality = constant:natural_disaster_family_lethality.earthquake }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_primary_building = token:infrastructure }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_secondary_building = token:industrial_complex }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_tertiary_building = token:arms_factory }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_default_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.aftershock }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_transport = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_industry = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_ports = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_air = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_resources = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_shelter = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_report_event_id = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_news_event_id = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_fraction = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_points = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_modifier_days = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_supply = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_supply_impact = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_speed = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_attrition = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_repair = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_resources = 0 }
			natural_disaster_prepare_family_profile = yes
			natural_disaster_prepare_warning_route_profile = yes
			natural_disaster_prepare_severity_profile = yes
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_impact_scale = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.baseline_impact_scale }
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution > 1 } }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_impact_scale = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.evolution_ii_impact_scale }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution > 2 } }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_impact_scale = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.evolution_iii_impact_scale }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_death_outcome = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_building_outcome = 1 }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_disruption_outcome = 1 }
			natural_disaster_prepare_chain_outcome_scaling = yes
			set_temp_variable = { nd_effect_death_scale = natural_disaster_death_scale }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_effect_death_scale = natural_disaster_chain_impact_scale }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_effect_death_scale = natural_disaster_chain_death_outcome }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_effect_building_scale = natural_disaster_building_scale }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_effect_building_scale = natural_disaster_chain_impact_scale }
			multiply_temp_variable = { nd_effect_building_scale = natural_disaster_chain_building_outcome }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_original_supply_scale = natural_disaster_supply_scale }
			multiply_variable = { natural_disaster_supply_scale = natural_disaster_chain_impact_scale }
			multiply_variable = { natural_disaster_supply_scale = natural_disaster_chain_disruption_outcome }

			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.aftershock }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.tsunami }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.lahar }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.wildfire_spread }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.famine }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.disease }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.refugee_pressure }
					}
				}
				natural_disaster_apply_population_loss = yes
			}
			natural_disaster_achievement_track_chain_arrival_after = yes
			if = {
				limit = {
					OR = {
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.aftershock }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.tsunami }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.lahar }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.wildfire_spread }
						check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.supply_collapse }
					}
				}
				natural_disaster_apply_family_building_damage = yes
			}
			natural_disaster_apply_state_disruption = yes
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_supply_scale = natural_disaster_chain_original_supply_scale }
			natural_disaster_schedule_family_report = yes
			set_temp_variable = { nd_effect_death_scale = 0 }
			set_temp_variable = { nd_effect_building_scale = 0 }
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { nd_achievement_chain_type = constant:natural_disaster_chain.refugee_pressure }
					check_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution > 1 }
					NOT = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_refugee_disease_pending }
				}
				set_state_flag = natural_disaster_refugee_disease_pending
				set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.disease }
				set_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.shelter_disease }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_ceiling = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_disease_days }
				add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_random_ceiling = 1 }
				set_temp_variable_to_random = {
					var = natural_disaster_refugee_disease_delay
					min = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.chain_disease_min_days
					max = natural_disaster_random_ceiling
					integer = yes
				}
				clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_arrived
				clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_partial_prevention
				clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_mission_success
				clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_mission_failed
				clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_stabilize_chain_prevention_taken
				set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_prevention_score = 0 }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_type = constant:natural_disaster_job_type.followup }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_delay_days = natural_disaster_refugee_disease_delay }
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_job_due_date = 0 }
				natural_disaster_enqueue_state_job = yes
				set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_due_date = natural_disaster_job_due_date }
				controller = { natural_disaster_activate_family_chain_mission = yes }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = { nd_achievement_chain_type = constant:natural_disaster_chain.disease }
					has_state_flag = natural_disaster_refugee_disease_pending
				}
				clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_refugee_disease_pending
			}
			if = {
				limit = { NOT = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_refugee_disease_pending } }
				clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_partial_prevention
				clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_mission_success
				clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_mission_failed
				clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_stabilize_chain_prevention_taken
				set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_prevention_score = 0 }
			}
		}
		if = {
			limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.none } }
			clear_variable = natural_disaster_chain_target_state
			clear_variable = natural_disaster_chain_family
			clear_variable = natural_disaster_chain_source_severity
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				natural_disaster_state_has_open_card = yes
				check_variable = { natural_disaster_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.late_reconstruction }
				NOT = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_only_card }
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_reassessment_delay = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.cleanup_reassessment_days }
			natural_disaster_schedule_reassessment_for_state = yes
		}
		if = {
			limit = {
				OR = {
					has_state_flag = natural_disaster_ocean_impact_proxy
					has_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_only_card
				}
			}
			natural_disaster_close_aftermath_card = yes
		}
		controller = { natural_disaster_refresh_category_display_family = yes }
	}
}

natural_disaster_apply_terminal_failure_consequence = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = natural_disaster_family }
	if = {
		limit = { NOT = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_terminal_failure_consequence } }
		set_state_flag = natural_disaster_terminal_failure_consequence
		subtract_from_variable = { natural_disaster_reconstruction_score = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.terminal_failure_score_penalty }
		multiply_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_supply = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.failed_recovery_scale }
		multiply_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_supply_impact = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.failed_recovery_scale }
		multiply_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_speed = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.failed_recovery_scale }
		multiply_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_attrition = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.failed_recovery_scale }
		multiply_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_repair = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.failed_recovery_scale }
		multiply_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_resources = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.failed_recovery_scale }
		if = {
			limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_unresolved_chain = yes }
			set_state_flag = natural_disaster_chain_mission_failed
		}
		else = {
			if = {
				limit = {
					check_variable = {
						var = natural_disaster_last_deaths
						value = constant:natural_disaster_achievement.second_wave_major_death_spike
						compare = greater_than_or_equals
					}
				}
				set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.political_shock }
				set_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.unrest }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_resources > 0 } }
				set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.famine }
				set_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.famine_pressure }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_shelter > 0 } }
				set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.refugee_pressure }
				set_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.refugee_pressure }
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_transport > 0 } }
				set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.supply_collapse }
				set_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.supply_collapse }
			}
			else = {
				set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_risk = constant:natural_disaster_chain.political_shock }
				set_variable = { natural_disaster_followup_route = constant:natural_disaster_followup_route.unrest }
			}
			natural_disaster_schedule_chain_followup = yes
		}
		natural_disaster_apply_state_disruption = yes
		natural_disaster_schedule_family_report = yes
	}
}

natural_disaster_finish_reconstruction_or_wait_for_chain = {
	if = {
		limit = { natural_disaster_state_chain_cleanup_ready = yes }
		natural_disaster_close_aftermath_card = yes
	}
	else = {
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.recovering }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_reassessment_delay = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.cleanup_reassessment_days }
		natural_disaster_schedule_reassessment_for_state = yes
	}
}

natural_disaster_execute_reassessment = {
	event_target:natural_disaster_impact_state = {
		if = {
			limit = {
				natural_disaster_state_has_open_card = yes
				NOT = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_recovery_unresolved_territory }
			}
			if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.early_rescue } }
				clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_phase_partial
				clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_phase_failed
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							var = natural_disaster_rescue_score
							value = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.rescue_success_score
							compare = greater_than_or_equals
						}
					}
					set_state_flag = natural_disaster_rescue_success
					set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.recovering }
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							var = natural_disaster_rescue_score
							value = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.rescue_partial_score
							compare = greater_than_or_equals
						}
					}
					set_state_flag = natural_disaster_phase_partial
					set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.degraded }
				}
				else = {
					set_state_flag = natural_disaster_phase_failed
					set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.degraded }
				}
				natural_disaster_release_state_mission_slots = yes
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.middle_stabilization }
			controller = {
				natural_disaster_fill_open_aftermath_capacity = yes
			}
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_reassessment_delay = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.stabilization_days }
				natural_disaster_schedule_reassessment_for_state = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.middle_stabilization } }
				clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_phase_partial
				clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_phase_failed
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							var = natural_disaster_stabilization_score
							value = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.stabilization_success_score
							compare = greater_than_or_equals
						}
					}
					set_state_flag = natural_disaster_stabilization_success
					multiply_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_supply = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.successful_stabilization_relief }
					multiply_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_repair = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.successful_stabilization_relief }
					set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.recovering }
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							var = natural_disaster_stabilization_score
							value = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.stabilization_partial_score
							compare = greater_than_or_equals
						}
					}
					set_state_flag = natural_disaster_phase_partial
					multiply_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_supply = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.partial_stabilization_relief }
					multiply_variable = { natural_disaster_mod_repair = constant:natural_disaster_chain_factor.partial_stabilization_relief }
					set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.degraded }
				}
				else = {
					set_state_flag = natural_disaster_phase_failed
					set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.degraded }
				}
				natural_disaster_release_state_mission_slots = yes
			set_variable = { natural_disaster_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.late_reconstruction }
			controller = {
				natural_disaster_fill_open_aftermath_capacity = yes
			}
				set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_reassessment_delay = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.reconstruction_days }
				natural_disaster_schedule_reassessment_for_state = yes
			}
			else_if = {
				limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.late_reconstruction } }
				if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							var = natural_disaster_reconstruction_score
							value = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.reconstruction_success_score
							compare = greater_than_or_equals
						}
					}
					set_state_flag = natural_disaster_reconstruction_success
					natural_disaster_commit_full_resilience = yes
					natural_disaster_finish_reconstruction_or_wait_for_chain = yes
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = {
						check_variable = {
							var = natural_disaster_reconstruction_score
							value = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.reconstruction_partial_score
							compare = greater_than_or_equals
						}
						NOT = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_reconstruction_partial }
					}
					set_state_flag = natural_disaster_reconstruction_partial
					natural_disaster_commit_partial_resilience = yes
					set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.recovering }
					set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_reassessment_delay = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.cleanup_reassessment_days }
					natural_disaster_schedule_reassessment_for_state = yes
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_reconstruction_partial }
					natural_disaster_finish_reconstruction_or_wait_for_chain = yes
				}
				else_if = {
					limit = { NOT = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_terminal_failure_consequence } }
					set_state_flag = natural_disaster_phase_failed
					set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.degraded }
					natural_disaster_clear_pending_resilience = yes
					natural_disaster_release_state_mission_slots = yes
					natural_disaster_apply_terminal_failure_consequence = yes
					set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_reassessment_delay = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.cleanup_reassessment_days }
					natural_disaster_schedule_reassessment_for_state = yes
				}
				else = {
					if = {
						limit = { natural_disaster_state_chain_cleanup_ready = yes }
						natural_disaster_close_aftermath_card = yes
					}
					else = {
						set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_reassessment_delay = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.cleanup_reassessment_days }
						natural_disaster_schedule_reassessment_for_state = yes
					}
				}
			}
		}
		else_if = {
			limit = {
				natural_disaster_state_has_open_card = yes
				has_state_flag = natural_disaster_recovery_unresolved_territory
			}
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_reassessment_delay = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.cleanup_reassessment_days }
			natural_disaster_schedule_reassessment_for_state = yes
		}
		natural_disaster_achievement_track_reassessment = yes
		controller = { natural_disaster_refresh_category_display_family = yes }
	}
}

natural_disaster_prepare_neighbor_profile = {
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.abnormal } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_neighbor_limit = constant:natural_disaster_damage.abnormal_neighbor_count }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_neighbor_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.catastrophic }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_neighbor_damage_points = constant:natural_disaster_damage.evolution_iii_neighbor_points }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.catastrophic } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_neighbor_limit = constant:natural_disaster_damage.catastrophic_neighbor_count }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_neighbor_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.regional }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_neighbor_damage_points = constant:natural_disaster_damage.evolution_ii_neighbor_points }
	}
	else = {
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_neighbor_limit = constant:natural_disaster_damage.regional_neighbor_count }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_neighbor_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.severe }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_neighbor_damage_points = constant:natural_disaster_damage.evolution_ii_neighbor_points }
	}
}

natural_disaster_apply_neighbor_impact = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_neighbor_had_open_card = 0 }
	if = {
		limit = { natural_disaster_state_has_open_card = yes }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_neighbor_had_open_card = 1 }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_neighbor_had_open_card < 1 } }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_total_deaths = 0 }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_death_driver = constant:natural_disaster_death_driver.none }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_foreign_relief_status = constant:natural_disaster_relief_status.none }
	}
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_id = nd_parent_sequence_id }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_impact_index = nd_parent_impact_index }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_family = natural_disaster_current_family }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_primary_family = natural_disaster_current_family }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_origin_family = nd_parent_origin_family }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_origin_medium = nd_parent_origin_medium }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_skyfall_variant = nd_parent_skyfall_variant }
	if = {
		limit = { has_event_target = natural_disaster_spread_origin_state }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_origin_state = event_target:natural_disaster_spread_origin_state }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_origin_family = natural_disaster_active_origin_family }
	}
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_path_basin = natural_disaster_sequence_path_basin }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_path_basin_family = natural_disaster_sequence_path_family }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_planned_hits = nd_parent_planned_hits }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_severity = natural_disaster_neighbor_severity }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution = natural_disaster_current_evolution }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_news_policy = constant:natural_disaster_news_policy.none }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_report_policy = nd_parent_report_policy }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_aftermath_policy = nd_parent_aftermath_policy }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_policy = constant:natural_disaster_chain_policy.none }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_neighbor_death_scale = nd_parent_death_scale }
	multiply_temp_variable = { nd_neighbor_death_scale = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.neighbor_falloff }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_neighbor_building_scale = nd_parent_building_scale }
	multiply_temp_variable = { nd_neighbor_building_scale = constant:natural_disaster_death_fraction.neighbor_falloff }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_death_scale = nd_neighbor_death_scale }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_building_scale = nd_neighbor_building_scale }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_recovery_scale = nd_parent_recovery_scale }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_caller_country = event_target:nd_parent_caller_country }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_preparation_score = 0 }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_outcome = 0 }
	set_state_flag = natural_disaster_neighbor_aftermath
	set_state_flag = natural_disaster_impact_scheduled
	set_temp_variable = { nd_achievement_sequence_id = nd_parent_sequence_id }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_call_severity = natural_disaster_neighbor_severity }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_planned_origin_family = nd_parent_origin_family }
	OWNER = { natural_disaster_achievement_begin_sequence = yes }

	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity = natural_disaster_neighbor_severity }
	natural_disaster_prepare_warning_route_profile = yes
	natural_disaster_prepare_severity_profile = yes
	air_contamination_register_natural_disaster_source = yes
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_points = natural_disaster_neighbor_damage_points }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_effect_death_scale = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_effect_building_scale = 0 }
	natural_disaster_apply_population_loss = yes
	natural_disaster_apply_family_building_damage = yes
	natural_disaster_achievement_track_impact = yes
	natural_disaster_activate_aftermath_for_state = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_aftermath_policy = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath_policy.none } }
		clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_neighbor_aftermath
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.closed }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_phase = constant:natural_disaster_phase.closed }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_cleanup_state = 1 }
	}
	natural_disaster_schedule_family_report = yes
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_impact_scheduled
}

natural_disaster_apply_regional_spread = {
	set_temp_variable = { nd_parent_impact_index = natural_disaster_impact_index }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_active_origin_saved = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_active_origin_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.random }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_path_basin = constant:natural_disaster_path_basin.none }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_path_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.random }
	natural_disaster_prepare_persisted_geography_context = yes
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_active_origin_saved < 1 } }
		save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_active_origin_state
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_active_origin_saved = 1 }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_active_origin_family = natural_disaster_current_family }
	}
	event_target:natural_disaster_active_origin_state = { save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_spread_origin_state }
	clear_temp_array = natural_disaster_neighbor_candidates
	every_neighbor_state = {
		limit = { natural_disaster_is_valid_family_target = yes }
		add_to_temp_array = {
			array = natural_disaster_neighbor_candidates
			value = THIS
		}
	}
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_neighbor_limit = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_neighbor_severity = constant:natural_disaster_severity.local }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_neighbor_damage_points = 0 }
	natural_disaster_prepare_neighbor_profile = yes
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_neighbors_applied = 0 }
	while_loop_effect = {
		limit = {
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_neighbors_applied < natural_disaster_neighbor_limit }
			check_variable = { natural_disaster_neighbor_candidates^num > 0 }
		}
		random_scope_in_array = {
			array = natural_disaster_neighbor_candidates
			save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_neighbor_state
			remove_from_temp_array = {
				array = natural_disaster_neighbor_candidates
				value = THIS
			}
			natural_disaster_apply_neighbor_impact = yes
		}
		add_to_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_neighbors_applied = 1 }
	}
}

natural_disaster_execute_repeated_impact = {
	# A selected-state repeat explicitly supersedes the visible card while
	# retaining its completed work as score. The fresh impact adds burden,
	# damage, deaths, reports, and chains through the same public runtime.
	natural_disaster_update_abnormal_history_record = yes
	natural_disaster_achievement_track_card_close = yes
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_id = natural_disaster_repeat_sequence_id }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_impact_index = natural_disaster_repeat_impact_index }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_family = natural_disaster_repeat_family }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_primary_family = natural_disaster_repeat_family }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_origin_family = natural_disaster_repeat_origin_family }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_origin_medium = natural_disaster_repeat_origin_medium }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_skyfall_variant = natural_disaster_repeat_skyfall_variant }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_planned_hits = natural_disaster_repeat_planned_hits }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_display_severity = natural_disaster_severity }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_severity > natural_disaster_repeat_display_severity } }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_display_severity = natural_disaster_repeat_severity }
	}
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_severity = natural_disaster_repeat_severity }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_evolution = natural_disaster_repeat_evolution }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_news_policy = natural_disaster_repeat_news_policy }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_report_policy = natural_disaster_repeat_report_policy }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_aftermath_policy = natural_disaster_repeat_aftermath_policy }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_policy = natural_disaster_repeat_chain_policy }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_death_scale = natural_disaster_repeat_death_scale }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_building_scale = natural_disaster_repeat_building_scale }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_recovery_scale = natural_disaster_repeat_recovery_scale }
	if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_aftermath_policy = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath_policy.light } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_recovery_scale = constant:natural_disaster_recovery_scale.light }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_aftermath_policy = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath_policy.full } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_recovery_scale = constant:natural_disaster_recovery_scale.full }
	}
	else_if = {
		limit = { check_variable = { natural_disaster_repeat_aftermath_policy = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath_policy.emergency } }
		multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_recovery_scale = constant:natural_disaster_recovery_scale.emergency }
	}
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_recovery_scale = natural_disaster_resolved_recovery_scale }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_supply_scale = natural_disaster_repeat_supply_scale }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_caller_country = natural_disaster_repeat_caller_country }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_schedule_date = natural_disaster_repeat_schedule_date }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_path_segment_index = natural_disaster_repeat_impact_index }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_path_status = constant:natural_disaster_path_status.impacted }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_outcome = 0 }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_choice = constant:natural_disaster_warning_choice.none }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_protection = constant:natural_disaster_warning_protection.none }
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_evolution_ii_card_sync_applied
	subtract_from_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_score = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.terminal_failure_score_penalty }
	subtract_from_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_score = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.terminal_failure_score_penalty }
	subtract_from_variable = { natural_disaster_reconstruction_score = constant:natural_disaster_aftermath.terminal_failure_score_penalty }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_recovery_burden = natural_disaster_resolved_recovery_scale }
	subtract_from_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_recovery_burden = 1.00 }
	multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_recovery_burden = 2.00 }
	round_temp_variable = natural_disaster_recovery_burden
	multiply_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_recovery_burden = -1 }
	add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_rescue_score = natural_disaster_recovery_burden }
	add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_stabilization_score = natural_disaster_recovery_burden }
	add_to_variable = { natural_disaster_reconstruction_score = natural_disaster_recovery_burden }
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_repeat_impact_scheduled
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_impact_scheduled
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_card_state = constant:natural_disaster_card_state.active }
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_impact_date = global.date }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_family = natural_disaster_family }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_severity = natural_disaster_repeat_severity }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_current_evolution = natural_disaster_evolution }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_news_policy = natural_disaster_news_policy }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_report_policy = natural_disaster_report_policy }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_aftermath_policy = natural_disaster_aftermath_policy }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_chain_policy = natural_disaster_chain_policy }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_death_scale = natural_disaster_death_scale }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_building_scale = natural_disaster_building_scale }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_news_cooldown_days = constant:natural_disaster_sequence.news_family_cooldown_days }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_transport = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_industry = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_ports = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_air = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_resources = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_shelter = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_damage_points = 0 }
	natural_disaster_prepare_family_profile = yes
	natural_disaster_prepare_warning_route_profile = yes
	natural_disaster_prepare_severity_profile = yes
	air_contamination_register_natural_disaster_source = yes
	set_temp_variable = { nd_effect_death_scale = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { nd_effect_building_scale = 0 }
	natural_disaster_apply_population_loss = yes
	natural_disaster_apply_family_building_damage = yes
	natural_disaster_achievement_track_impact = yes
	natural_disaster_resolve_causal_chain_risk = yes
	natural_disaster_apply_state_disruption = yes
	natural_disaster_schedule_chain_followup = yes
	natural_disaster_schedule_family_report = yes
	natural_disaster_schedule_family_news = yes
	natural_disaster_register_abnormal_gui_card = yes
	set_variable = { natural_disaster_severity = natural_disaster_repeat_display_severity }
	natural_disaster_set_category_display_from_state = yes
	controller = {
		set_country_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_unread
		set_country_flag = natural_disaster_aftermath_category_visible
		natural_disaster_fill_open_aftermath_capacity = yes
	}
}

# State scope. Restores the physical origin and path-domain proof persisted on
# a delayed card before the worker revalidates it.
natural_disaster_prepare_persisted_geography_context = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_active_origin_saved = 0 }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_active_origin_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.random }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_active_origin_medium = constant:natural_disaster_origin_medium.none }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_origin_state }
		var:natural_disaster_origin_state = { save_event_target_as = natural_disaster_active_origin_state }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_active_origin_saved = 1 }
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_origin_family }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_active_origin_family = natural_disaster_origin_family }
		}
		if = {
			limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_origin_medium }
			set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_active_origin_medium = natural_disaster_origin_medium }
		}
	}
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_path_basin = constant:natural_disaster_path_basin.none }
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_path_family = constant:natural_disaster_family.random }
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_path_basin }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_path_basin = natural_disaster_path_basin }
	}
	if = {
		limit = { has_variable = natural_disaster_path_basin_family }
		set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_sequence_path_family = natural_disaster_path_basin_family }
	}
}

# State scope. A sea impact is represented by the nearest exposed coast only as
# an observation and warning anchor. The proxy receives no crater casualties,
# building damage, disruption modifier, or reconstruction burden. Its sole live
# objective is the delayed, basin-locked tsunami chain.
natural_disaster_execute_ocean_impact_proxy = {
	set_temp_variable = { natural_disaster_resolved_chain = constant:natural_disaster_chain.none }
	if = {
		limit = { has_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_active }
		set_variable = { natural_disaster_warning_outcome = constant:natural_disaster_warning.timely_outcome }
	}
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_active
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_warning_scheduled
	clr_state_flag = natural_disaster_impact_scheduled
